# DOCUMENT 1: COMPLETE GAME DESIGN DOCUMENT (GDD)
**Project Title:** *Infinite Tower: Become a Master!*  
**Target Platform:** Roblox (PC, Mobile, Tablet, Console)  
**Server Capacity:** 8 Players per Server (8 Dedicated Floating Fortress Plots around the Cloister Commons)  
**Engine Architecture:** Server-Authoritative Data Tables (10 Hz Heartbeat, 5 Hz tower snapshots) + Client-Side R15 Visual Interpolation (60 FPS `RenderStepped`) | Hybrid Replication: Central Hub 14-Byte Binary Buffers + Infinite Tower Room-Local v2 Binary Buffers (24-byte entity records, 46-byte header)

---

**Terms used throughout:** the **Skill Gauge** (0–100) charges with attacks and fires a hero's Ultimate; the code calls it `Stamina` / `CurrentStamina` and it travels as the `Stamina` byte of the tower protocol. A hero at 0 HP is **Downed** (sim state `COMA`) until the squad changes floor, goes home or wipes. A **Relic** is the only physical loot.

## 1. EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW & PLAYER FANTASY

### 1.1 High-Concept Pitch
**Infinite Tower: Become a Master!** is a tactical squad-command Action-RPG, extraction sandbox, and fortress progression game inspired by the dark survival manhwa *Pick Me Up, Infinite Gacha*. Players do not play as generic hack-and-slash warriors; instead, each player embodies a **Master**—a dimensional overseer who summons, trains, equips, promotes, and commands a roster of sentient Gacha Heroes from a private floating sanctuary known as the **Master's Fortress Plot**.

### 1.2 The "Pick Me Up" Fantasy & Zero-Waste Roster Economy
In traditional gacha/simulator games, 95% of summons are discarded as worthless stat-sticks, and combat is reduced to passive AFK auto-clicking. **Infinite Tower** solves both problems through two foundational pillars:

1. **The Dual-Role Living Roster (Combat + Labor):**
   - Every hero pulled from the Summoning Altar rolls with **Combat Stats** (movement speed, attack speed, attack damage, health) and **Labor Output** (separate facility productivity). Heroes have no passive traits: rarity, stars, level and their kit (§4.4) set them apart.
   - **Up to 5 Deployed Heroes** (one slot at the start; the Fortress Tree's Command branch opens slots 2–5, see §4.3) follow the Master onto floors 1–50 of the **Infinite Tower** or are assigned to a floor on their own. Heroes charge an in-combat **Skill Gauge** (0 to 100) through a 3-tier attack cadence (Basic -> Basic -> Special -> Skill Attack at 100), and risk being **Downed** (until the squad changes floor, goes home or wipes) if left inside lethal AoE telegraphs.
   - **Benched Heroes** rest on the player's **Fortress Plot** or work in its support facilities (§4.3).
   - **Zero Dead Pulls:** Duplicate heroes across all star ranks are fed into the **Synthesis Chamber** (`SynthesisController.luau`) for EXP leveling to the star cap (Lv. 10 at every star); capped heroes then rank up at the **Promotion Altar** (`PromotionController.luau`) with Gold and the Promotion Stones that tower kills and relics drop (**Star Promotion**, $1★ \to 7★$, §4.2a). Both heroes and artifacts feature a baseline inventory space limit of **50 items**, raised by the Fortress Tree's Storage branch (§4.3). Heroes working in a support facility are out of the backpack and don't count toward the hero cap.
   - **Dual Hero Banners & Open Forge 3D Rolling:** At the **Summoning Altar**, players spend Gems on two distinct hero banners (Call of the Rift `100` / `900` Gems, A Star's Arrival `200` / `1,800` Gems for 1x / 10x): **Call of the Rift** (Permanent Banner featuring 7 stars: $1★ \to 7★$) and **A Star's Arrival** (Limited Time Banner featuring 1 character per star across all 7 stars: $1★ \to 7★$). For weapons, Masters visit the open-air **Open Forge** (`Building_Blacksmith`): strike or press `[F]` on the **Master Anvil** to reroll unlocked artifact pedestals **for free**, inspect the spinning 3D artifacts with overhead rarity + name labels, and walk up to a pedestal to purchase that **1★** weapon for **Relic-floor scaled Gold** (`625 Gold` Common up to `2.28M Gold` Mythical Enhanced across the **84-Weapon Roster**). At the **Forge Smelter** (`ForgeWorkbenchController.luau`), donor weapons of the same rarity and star rank (regardless of weapon type), plus Gold, are used for **Star Ascension** ($1★ \to 7★$), scaling base stats up to **`16.00x`**, while unneeded artifacts can be **sold for Gold**.

2. **Tactical Master Agency Over AFK Whaling:**
   - The Master carries no personal weapon. Instead, the Master directs squads through battlefield commands—**`[Click / Tap]` Focus Target**, **`[Q]` Overdrive Whistle**, and **`[E]` Emergency Recall Whistle**—while physically hauling heavy, high-value **Relics** back to a Sanctuary or their plot.
   - Masters use **`[LeftShift]` Sprint** ($3.0\times$ speed multiplier) to cross high-elevation bridges and dodge lethal hazards.
   - Because boss signature skill checks (the **Biome Boss** signature attacks like *Moss Quake* and *Sweeping Roots*, and elite ground telegraphs) hit every hero left inside the blast zone for heavy damage, often enough to leave them **Downed**, an AFK player will have their squad wiped and sent home, dropping their DPS to zero. An active Master who times **`[E]` Emergency Recall Whistle** to dodge the blast and triggers **`[Q]` Overdrive Whistle** during punish windows will consistently out-damage and out-loot an AFK whale.

### 1.3 Hybrid Retention Matrix & Core Drivers

| Reference Title | Adopted Core Mechanic | *Infinite Tower* Evolution |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| ***Pick Me Up, Infinite Gacha*** | Desperate squad survival, Synthesis chamber, Star Promotion, living lobby workers | Heroes are Downed if mismanaged; high-star promotions need hard sacrifices; War Banner plot deployment. |
| ***Pet Simulator 99*** | Multi-unit squad grinding, tactile loot explosions, exponential progression | Replaces passive pet hovering with Skill-Gauge-driven combat squads, active whistle commands, and isolated 50-floor dynamic tower rooms. |
| ***Blox Fruits*** | Telegraphed boss skills, live DPS leaderboards, open-world bounty hunting | **Greed vs. Coma** boss checks, 10 **Biome Bosses** (Floors 5, 10, ..., 50), and **Contested Loot & Bounty Duels** on tower floors. |
| ***Grow a Garden*** | Cozy-yet-competitive perimeter plots, passive production, fortress management | Dedicated private plots with a Fortress Tree of unlockable facilities, War Banner squad deployment, and Forge progression. |
| ***Steal an Egg*** | Physical object hauling, movement penalties, fumble & steal tension | **Relics** weld overhead, slow WalkSpeed by up to -45%, and fumble on AoE hits. |

**The Intimate Server Dynamic:**
In a 10-player server, anonymity disappears. Every player knows who conquered the latest Biome Boss, who just scooped contested loot, and who carries an open 3-minute thief bounty. The interplay of high-stakes extraction, boss timing, and bounty-hunt loops keeps players engaged in continuous Biome Boss clears and Infinite Tower floor pushes.

## 2. DETAILED CORE LOOPS

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph Micro["3-Minute Loop: Grind & Extract"]
        M1["Deploy up to 5 Heroes (Tower Floors 1-50)"] --> M2["Command Squad: Focus Target, [Q] Overdrive, [E] Recall Dodge"]
        M2 --> M3["Carry a Relic (-15% to -45% WalkSpeed)"]
        M3 --> M4["Haul it to the Floor Sanctuary or a Hub Bank Zone (Dodge AoE & Loot Thieves)"]
        M4 --> M5["Bank Gold & Gems; Swap Heroes at the War Banner"]
        M5 --> M1
    end

    subgraph Meso["15-Minute Loop: Summoning, Synthesis, Promotion & Forge"]
        T1["Gather Gold & Gems from Drops, Relics & Tower Floors"] --> T2["Summoning Altar: Call of the Rift (1-7★ Perm) & A Star's Arrival (1-7★ Limited)"]
        T2 --> T3["Synthesis Chamber: Feed Duplicates for XP & Max Level Cap"]
        T1 --> T4["Open Forge: Free Anvil Rerolls & 3D Artifact Pedestals (Up to 6)"]
        T3 --> T5["Promotion Altar: Sacrifice Same-Rank Heroes for Star Promotion (1★ -> 7★)"]
        T4 --> T6["Forge Smelter: Star Ascension (1★ -> 7★ Multipliers) & Gold Liquidation"]
    end

    subgraph Macro["Macro Loop: Tower Progression & Biome Boss Conquests"]
        B1["Prepare Squad: Promote Heroes & Equip High-Rarity/Star Artifacts"] --> B2["Advance Tower Floors: Clear Mobs & Elites across 5-Floor Biome"]
        B2 --> B3["Engage Biome Boss on Floor Milestone (Floor 5, 10, 15... 50)"]
        B3 --> B4["Dodge Signature Telegraphed AoEs (Self/Target Discs) via [E] Recall"]
        B4 --> B5["Punish Window: Trigger [Q] Overdrive to Burst Down Boss HP"]
        B5 --> B6["Claim Milestone Rewards: Massive Gold & Gems Currency Payouts"]
        B6 --> B7["Unlock Next Biome Tier & Advance to Higher Tower Floors"]
    end

    Micro --> Meso
    Meso --> Macro
    Macro --> Micro
```

### 2.1 The 3-Minute Micro Loop (Grind, Command & Physical Extraction)
1. **Deploy & Traverse (0:00 – 0:15):**
   - The Master selects up to 5 rested heroes from their roster via the **War Banner** or **Squad Deploy UI** (`SquadController.luau`).
   - The Master sprints (`[LeftShift]`, $3.0\times$ speed) from their plot across the Cloister Commons and the Pilgrim's Causeway to the tower gate, then uses the **Tower Controller** (`TowerController.client.luau`) to travel (`Visit floor`) or station their squad (`Assign squad`) on any unlocked floor of the 50 **Infinite Tower Floors**. The hub itself has no mobs.
2. **Tactical Grind & Skill Gauge Building (0:15 – 2:00):**
   - The Master uses **`[Click / Tap]` Focus Target** to direct every deployed hero onto a target mob or floor boss.
   - Heroes execute a 3-tier cadence: **Basic Attack** (+5 Skill Gauge) $\to$ **Special Attack** (+10 Skill Gauge every 3rd hit) $\to$ **Skill Attack** (unleashed when the gauge reaches 100, resetting it to 0).
   - To accelerate clears and rapidly charge Skill Attacks, the Master triggers **`[Q]` Overdrive Whistle** (`20s CD`), granting **+40% Attack Speed** for `5s` at no cost.
   - When an Elite or Boss telegraphs a red ground AoE or ranged projectile (`ARC`, `SELF_DISC`, `TARGET_DISC`, or swept-corridor `PROJECTILE`), the Master triggers **`[E]` Emergency Recall Whistle** (`6s CD`), warping all 5 heroes to the Master's safe position and dropping their aggro for `2s`.
3. **Relic Haul (2:00 – 2:45):**
   - Defeating mobs, Elites and bosses spawns physical Relic drops in `Workspace.PhysicalRelics`: **Mana Geodes** and **Cursed Urns** from anything, **Abyssal Cores** only from Elites, and **Calamity Hearts** only from Alphas and Biome Bosses (see §5.5 Relic Drops).
   - The Master holds the drop's **Carry** prompt (E, 0.35 s) to weld it overhead, incurring a **-15% to -45% WalkSpeed penalty** (and disabling jumping for high-tier relics). Relics are never picked up by walking over them.
   - If hit by an Elite/Boss AoE mid-haul, the Master is **stunned for 1s and fumbles the item** (which becomes stealable after a **5s owner-lock**).
4. **Extract & Bank (2:45 – 3:00):**
   - **Hub Banking:** Stepping onto the Cloister Commons, a plot bridge, the Pilgrim's Causeway, your plot, or a Private Extraction Pad safely banks a carried relic into permanent Gold and Gems.
   - **Floor Sanctuary Banking:** Carrying a relic into any floor's elevated **Sanctuary** banks it automatically anywhere inside (there is no pad or altar), with the full relic payout and the Market multiplier, without a trip back to the Hub. Kill Gold and Gems never need banking: they are paid on the kill.
   - Downed heroes stay down until the squad changes floor (they stand back up at 30% HP), returns home or wipes. A wipe sends the Master and squad home at full HP (see §2.5 and §6.1).

### 2.2 The 15-Minute Meso Loop (Fortress, Synthesis, Promotion & Forge Progression)
1. **Roster Upkeep (Every 3–5 Minutes):**
   - Swap heroes between the squad, the bench and the support facilities at the War Banner and the facility panels. The squad is **locked** while the Master or the squad is in the tower (including a stationed squad and floor transitions) or in the raid arena: deploying, benching or reassigning a squad hero, Deploy Best and the exhausted-hero auto swap are all refused by the server until both are home. Bench and facility moves for heroes outside the squad still work. A refused squad change shows as a modal (`SquadLockModal`, keyed by the `SQUAD_LOCKED_TOWER` / `SQUAD_LOCKED_RAID` code `AssignHeroRole` returns); in the tower it offers **Return to Hub**.
2. **Summoning & Open Forge Rolling (Every 5–10 Minutes):**
   - Spend Gems at the **Summoning Altar** on either **Call of the Rift** (Common–Mythical Permanent Banner) or **A Star's Arrival** (Common–Mythical Limited Time Rate-Up Banner, featuring 1 character per rarity tier, each summoned at 1★). In addition, visit the **Open Forge** (`ForgeRollService.luau` / `ForgePedestalController.client.luau`) to reroll unlocked pedestals (1–6) **for free** at the Master Anvil (via click or `[F]`, or automatically with the **Forge Auto-Roll** GamePass) and walk to any pedestal to buy its rolled 3D weapon for **Relic-floor scaled Gold** across the **84-Weapon Roster**.
   - **Synthesis Chamber (`SynthesisController.luau`):** Place a recipient hero and fodder heroes to level it up to the current star's cap (Lv. 10).
   - **Promotion Altar (`PromotionController.luau`):** When a hero reaches max level, spend Gold and Promotion Stones to permanently ascend its star rating ($1★ \to 7★$, §4.2a). The hero's inherent rarity never changes.
3. **Artifact Star Ascension & Liquidation at the Forge Smelter (`ForgeWorkbenchController.luau`):**
   - **Star Ascension (Multipliers):** Ascend artifacts from $1★ \to 7★$ at the Forge Smelter by sacrificing donor weapons of the same rarity tier and star rank (any weapon type) and Gold, scaling inherent base stats by progressive multipliers up to **`16.00x`**.
   - **Artifact Liquidation (Selling):** Sell unequipped, unlocked weapons for Gold directly from the Smelter Workbench to maintain inventory space.
   - **Locking & odds disclosure:** Any weapon can be locked from its detail card; locked weapons can't be sold or picked by the sell quick-selects, and high-tier sales ask for confirmation. Inside the Smelter's **Odds & Prices** modal, Masters inspect live **Roll Luck**-boosted per-pedestal odds (uncapped via the `ForgeLuck` Fortress Tree branch) alongside Standard and Enhanced Relic-floor Gold prices for all 84 weapons.

### 2.3 The Macro Loop (Tower Progression & Biome Boss Conquests)
1. **Biome Traversal & Preparation:**
   - Masters organize their 5-hero squad, equip high-rarity and high-star artifacts, and rest exhausted heroes on the bench before entering a new Biome.
   - Each of the 10 biomes spans 5 progressively harder floors (50 floors total) with 4 unique biome enemy species (2 Regulars, 1 Elite, and 1 Biome Boss; 40 total enemy species across the Tower). On floors 1–4 of a biome the Elite is the summoned floor champion; on the 5th floor two Elite-led packs hold the Bastions. Enemy health and attack damage follow the stat-reference curve in Section 4.2. Reward scaling remains $\text{Reward} \times 1.055^{\text{Floor}-1}$; stat inflation does not change rewards.
2. **The Biome Boss Encounter (Floors 5, 10, 15, ..., 50):**
   - The 5th floor of every biome is a dedicated **Biome Boss Arena** (`BOSS_ARENA` layout variant) featuring an 8-column ritual ring, boss braziers, and a massive boss dais fixture (`BossDaisFixture`, $60 \times 2 \times 60\text{ studs}$).
   - Every Biome Boss transitions dynamically from **Phase 1** to **Phase 2 (Enraged)** below 55% HP and wields devastating signature attacks with distinct authoritative shapes and timings:
     - **Basic Attacks:** Wide frontal arcs (`ARC`, e.g. `guardian_crush`, 13 studs).
     - **Signature 1:** Expanding circular ground shocks centered on the boss (`SELF_DISC`, e.g. `moss_quake`, 22 studs radius).
     - **Signature 2:** Ranged ground craters placed under distant heroes (`TARGET_DISC`, e.g. `sweeping_roots`, 55 studs cast range, 13 studs radius).
     - **Ranged / Caster Regulars:** Biome ranged and caster regulars (`mob_lantern_spore`, `mob_crag_channeler`, `mob_thorn_weaver`, `mob_drowned_acolyte`, `mob_astral_scribe`, `mob_void_invoker`) fire authoritative swept-corridor projectiles (`PROJECTILE`) with a floor corridor warning and flying orb visual.
   - **Tactical Counterplay ("Dodge vs. Coma"):** Masters must watch the expanding progress-fill telegraph discs, corridor warnings, and sound cues, repositioning and executing **`[E]` Emergency Recall Whistle** to yank heroes out of danger. Heroes struck by signature skills take heavy damage and are often left **Downed** (until the squad changes floor, goes home or wipes).
   - **Punish Windows:** Following major signature attacks, the boss enters an extended recovery window (1.2s–1.5s), providing active Masters the ideal moment to pop **`[Q]` Overdrive Whistle** for massive burst DPS.
3. **Biome Clearance & Milestone Rewards:**
   - Defeating the Biome Boss awards immense Gold, Gems, and first-clear milestone bonuses.
   - Floors unlock in order: defeating a floor's boss opens the next floor on the **Floor Selector** (`TowerController.client.luau`). Only a Master standing on the floor can clear it; an assigned squad farms Gold, gems and XP but cannot climb.

### 2.4 The AFK & Outskirts Facility Loop (Passive Boost Facilities & Auto/Offline Gold Generation)
While active squad command in the Central Hub and Infinite Tower drives peak progression, the **Outskirts & Facility Loop** ensures that a Master's benched roster continuously generates value both online and offline—reinforcing the **Zero-Waste Roster Economy**.

1. **Inner Fortress Stations vs. The Rear Outskirts:**
   - **Core Main Buildings (Inner Courtyard):** Essential progression stations that every Master accesses by default in their main fortress courtyard—including the **Summoning Altar**, **War Banner**, **Synthesis Chamber**, **Promotion Altar** and **Blacksmith Forge**.
   - **Support Facilities (Inner Fortress Wings):** Upgradeable utility and economic structures (`Level 1` $\to$ `Level 5`) located inside the fortress courtyard wings where benched heroes are stationed via `FacilityController.luau` to grant global percentage buffs. Each is unlocked and levelled through the **Fortress Development Tree** (Section 4.3):
     - **God's Blessing (Plaza Fountain):** An angel statue on the courtyard fountain. Stationed Acolytes (`Healer` preferred) raise **summon luck** on every Summoning Altar banner ($+4\%$ at Lv. 1 up to a $+50\%$ cap).
     - **The Market (Inner Commercial Wing):** Grants a percentage multiplier ($+5\%$ base at Lv. 1 up to $+40\%+$ with high-tier Berserkers) **strictly to Loot Earnings from the Towers**: Gold from Infinite Tower kills (Floors $1\text{–}50$) and the Gold of banked relics. (It does not multiply Outskirts Guard Post passive Gold.)
     - **The Training Grounds (Inner Martial Wing):** Grants a percentage multiplier ($+4\%$ base at Lv. 1 up to $+32\%+$ with high-tier Strikers/Scholars) to the Master's **Overall Squad Combat Power**, multiplying total `FinalATK` and `FinalHP` (both intrinsic hero stats and equipped Artifact stats) against all monsters.
   - **The Outskirts (Southern Frontier Wilderness):** The only area located *outside* the fortress walls, reached through the rear fortress gatehouse. Houses the **Outskirts Guard Post** and its wave-defense trail.
2. **The Outskirts Guard Post (Unified AFK Gold & Offline Rewards):**
   - At the southern rear border of the island, outside the fortress battlements, lies the **Outskirts Guard Post**, a frontier bastion and wilderness trail at the back of the fortress.
   - Masters assign benched heroes (`Vanguard` and `Marksman` preferred) to the Outskirts Guard Post as **Adventurers**. Assigned Adventurers hold a defense arc against cosmetic mob waves in a lightweight client-rendered loop (§4.3, *Facility Workers in the World*); it costs them nothing.
   - **Auto-Received Online Gold:** There is no manual treasury chest required while online. Every $60\text{ seconds}$ (ticked automatically in $10\text{-second}$ increments), passive Gold generated by stationed Adventurers is **automatically added directly to the Master's Gold balance**, scaled by the heroes' `EffectiveLabor` and the Master's **Highest Cleared Biome Boss** (Floors $5, 10, \dots, 50$).
   - **Offline Reward Function (`OfflineRewardService.luau`):** When a Master logs out (`PlayerRemoving`), the server records `LastLogoutTimestamp` alongside their current `PassiveGoldPerMin` rate. Upon reconnecting (`PlayerAdded`), the server calculates elapsed offline minutes up to the Guard Post's **Max Offline Accumulation Cap** ($4\text{ Hours}$ at Lv. 1 $\to$ $12\text{ Hours}$ at Lv. 5) and presents a **"Welcome Back, Master!" Offline Summary Modal**, crediting the accumulated Gold straight to the player's balance.

### 2.5 The Tower Run Loop (Phase 6)
A **run** starts when a Master first stands on a tower floor and ends when they return to the hub or their plot, their squad wipes, or they disconnect. Spec: `docs/rework-plan/PHASE_6_TOWER_RUN_LOOP.md`.

1. **Floors keep moving.** A floor holds 16 / 22 / 25 / 35 / 35 live mobs (floors 1–5 of a biome) in 4–7 camps. Every camp mixes all of the biome's Regular types, about half the camps add one support Regular (Phase D), and the floor's 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 / 4 Elites lead randomly chosen camps (see the camp roster rule in §5). A camp respawns as one wave 10 s after its last member dies (a lone survivor cannot hold it back past 45 s). Each extra Master on the floor adds 40% more members to every camp, up to 48 live mobs. When a Master's kill progress crosses 50% (floors 1–4 only), a personal **surge pack** (1 Elite and 3 Regulars) steps out near their squad.
2. **A longer climb.** A floor boss is summoned after `round(20 + 1.2 × floor)` kill points (a Regular is 1, a camp Elite 3). The boss of floors 1–4 is the biome's **Alpha**, a larger, gilded elite with its own name, stats and a self-quake attack; the 5th floor is the **Biome Boss**. Enemy strength is anchored to the squad a player is expected to have, with a per-biome sawtooth (×0.85, 0.95, 1.05, 1.15, 1.30 by floor in the biome). Floors 1–9 keep their early-game relief curve (2 heroes at floor 1, 3 by floor 5), raised ×1.5 HP and ×1.25 ATK once a new Master's first ten-pull guaranteed a Superior, so the first biome is sized to a Superior-led pair rather than a Rare-led one. From floor 10 the anchors are built on today's hero rules (1★ summon, ×1.5 per promotion, Lv. 1–10 per star) in `StatBalance.ExpectedSquads`: floor 10 expects 4 heroes led by a **promoted (2★) Rare**, floor 30 a squad of about 3★ Exalteds, floor 45 6★ Legendaries/Mythicals, and **floor 50 a full squad of 7★ Lv. 10 Legendary and Mythical heroes** (two Mythicals, three Legendaries) with Legendary 5★ weapons. Enemy HP comes from that squad's summed DPS and enemy ATK from the geometric mean of its HP, so the curve gates on both damage and survival: an unpromoted Rare/Uncommon squad stalls at floor 9, a 5★ Legendary/Mythical squad in the last biome, and a 6★ squad cannot take the floor-50 boss even with Mythical 7★ weapons. Every tower floor then multiplies the curve by `StatBalance.TowerDifficulty`: enemy HP ×1.35 on all 50 floors and enemy ATK ×1.2, with the ATK bump tapering from floor 40 to ×1.0 at floor 50 so the final boss stays gated on the full 7★ squad surviving four hits. Raids read the unscaled curve for boss ATK. Floor 1 is sized to the default starter pair, and new profiles start with 900 Gems (one 10-pull).
3. **Penalties, never losing what you own.** A knocked-out hero stays down until the squad **changes floor** (it stands back up at 30% HP), returns home, or wipes. Floor changes no longer heal anyone; the tower sanctuary heals standing heroes (10% HP per second). After a floor's summoned boss has been damaged, the floor's travel stays sealed until it dies or the squad wipes. A **wipe** costs the carried relic, the streak and 25% of the Gold and Gems earned since the last bank. Heroes, weapons and banked currency are never lost.
4. **Banking and the climb streak.** Entering a sanctuary or pad banks everything. Each floor cleared in a row adds +10% (up to +100%) to the value of relics banked during the run; banking does not reset the streak, only the end of the run does. Relics are the contested loot: each enemy role has its own drop table (§5.5 Relic Drops), and relic Gems (base 100 / 300 / 1,000 / 5,000) and Gold both scale with the floor.
5. **Rift Attunement.** In the tower and in bounty duels, one hero's power is capped at about 1.25× an on-curve hero two floors above the Master's highest floor reached, so a lucky or premium pull is felt without skipping a biome. Damage ($10 \times DPS$) and HP are capped **separately**: each gets the hero's own archetype share of the cap (its seed HP, ATK and interval), and whatever one side doesn't use goes to the other, so the capped hero still lands exactly on the cap. A huge weapon therefore trims only damage and never drains the hero's HP (`StatFormulas.SplitAttunement`, `GetAttunedMultipliers`). Duels use the lower of the two Masters' caps. Facility labor is never capped, and the pull itself (hero, star, rarity) never changes.
6. **Assigned squads farm.** An assigned squad earns kill Gold, Gems and XP anywhere, but relics, kill progress, boss summons and floor unlocks need its Master standing on the floor.
7. **Simulation rate.** The tower simulation runs at 10 Hz (attack intervals round up to 0.1 s); snapshots to clients stay at 5 Hz until a Studio profile clears the bandwidth budget.

---

## 3. MAP & SPATIAL DESIGN SPECIFICATIONS

### 3.1 Dual-Mode Spatial Architecture

The world exists in two coordinated spatial contexts:
1. **The Central Hub & Cloister Commons (World Coordinates):** Landmark Central Crimson Tower (`TowerBuilder`: a 650-stud-wide dark-slate gothic drum of 16 window tiers in four gentle setbacks, each tier with crimson tracery windows and a balcony, under a lantern crown, eight pinnacle turrets and a great spire about 2,150 studs above its base; the Blender remodel's meshes replace the part-built look once imported, see `GothicTowerShell`), the Cloister Commons (a solid rounded-rectangle garden hub) in front of the tower gate, and the 8 player fortress plots around its outer edge. All hub geometry comes from `HubLayout.luau`.
2. **The 50 Dynamic Isolated Tower Floors (Off-Map Isolated Grid):** Server-managed dynamic encounter rooms for vertical climbing and Biome Boss arenas.

```
                    CENTRAL HUB: CLOISTER COMMONS & 8 FORTRESS PLOTS
                                     N (-Z)
                              PLOT 4      PLOT 5
                                ║           ║
                       ╭────────╨───────────╨────────╮
                       │   orchards, pool, portal    │
               PLOT 3 ═╡  ╲          │          ╱    ╞═ PLOT 6
                       │    ╲    ┌───┴───┐    ╱      │
               PLOT 2 ═╡══════════│ DAIS  │══════════╞═ PLOT 7     paths: each landing -> dais
                       │    ╱    └───┬───┘    ╲      │
               PLOT 1 ═╡  ╱  gazebo  │  pavilion ╲   ╞═ PLOT 8
                       │ overlook    │   biome stones│
                       ╰─────────────┬───────────────╯   hub 480 x 640, centre (0, 12, -796)
                                     │ Pilgrim's Causeway (90 long, 40 wide)
                              [ GATE (0,-332) ]
                         ( CENTRAL CRIMSON TOWER  0,0 )  walkway ring R = 375
                                      S (+Z)

               =======================================================
               OFF-MAP INFINITE TOWER ROOMS (Isolated Dynamic Grid)
               Origin(id) = (12000 + ((id-1)%10)*2400, 1000, 12000 + floor((id-1)/10)*2400)
               380 x 420 (floor 1 of a biome) to 560 x 640 studs (floor 5) chambers with Sanctuaries (CombatSafe=true)
               Floors 5, 10, 15, ..., 50 feature dedicated Biome Boss Dais Arenas (BossDaisFixture)
```

### 3.2 Global Coordinate Systems & Zones Table

| Zone Name | Radial Distance / Coordinate Space | Dimensions | Access Rule | Combat & PvP Rules | Primary Drops |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Central Crimson Tower** | Centred at `(0, 0)`, ~650-stud footprint, walkway ring at `R = 375` ($Y = 16$) | Central multi-level landmark on its round CrackedGroundSlab | Open to all | No mobs in the hub. The gate portal (north, `z = -332`) opens the floor selector for the Infinite Tower. | None |
| **Hub Banking Zones** | `HubLayout.BankZonePolygons()`: the Commons (its rounded outline), the 8 plot bridges and the Pilgrim's Causeway, plus a box around each PlotSpawn. The open air around them is never a bank zone | Authored polygons (`TowerFloorService`) | Open to all | **Safe Zone** (No mobs). Entering one banks a carried relic. | Relic banking |
| **The Cloister Commons** | Rounded rectangle centred at `(0, 12, -796)`: X ±240, Z −1116 to −476, corners R 60 | One solid 480 × 640 garden, with cobbled paths from the central Wayfinder Dais to every plot landing (Section 3.5) | Open to all | Safe Zone. The social hub every player crosses between their plot and the tower. | None |
| **Pilgrim's Causeway** | `z = -386` to `-476` on the gate axis | `40 studs` wide, slopes from $Y = 16$ to $Y = 12$ (`CausewayBuilder.luau`) | Open to all | Safe Zone. Joins the tower walkway to the cloister's South Gate (44 wide). An obsidian deck with a crimson rune channel whose glow rolls toward the tower, spiked parapets, three pairs of fire-crowned pylons joined by arcs of crimson energy, two crested arches with hanging cages, two hooded Warden statues at the tower end, and spikes, chains, embers and red mist hanging into the void below. | None |
| **8 Floating Fortress Plots** | Around the Commons' outer edge (`HubLayout.PlotSlots`): 3 per long side (island roots at x ±448, 208 apart), 2 on the north edge. Neighbours at least 24 studs apart, nearest point to the tower about 570 ($Y = 12$) | About $184 \times 234\text{ studs}$ citadel island (courtyard plus rear outskirts; `PlotHalfWidth`, `PlotFrontDepth`, `PlotBackDepth`); each faces into the hub and joins its edge by a 40-stud bridge | Plot Owner + Visitors | Safe Zone. Personal fortress sanctuary for recovery, banner summoning, forge ascension, and AFK outskirts guard post. Assigned nearest-to-the-gate first: 1, 8, 2, 7, 3, 6, 4, 5. | Roster Recovery, Gacha Banners, Forge, Outskirts Passive Gold & Multipliers |
| **Safety Catch Plane** | Lower kill plane at $Y = -95$, centred on `(0, -950)` | `2400 x 8 x 3400 studs`, tiled (parts cap at 2048) | Fallen players | Non-lethal catch plane; teleports fallen players safely to their PlotSpawn. | None |
| **50 Tower Floor Rooms** | Off-map grid: $X \ge 12,000, Y = 1,000, Z \ge 12,000$ (spaced $2,400\text{ studs}$) | Progressive $380 \times 130 \times 420\text{ studs}$ (F1) to $560 \times 190 \times 640\text{ studs}$ (F5) across 5 elevation tiers | Traversal via `TowerController` | Isolated floor-local PvE encounters; shared floor membership; elevated Sanctuary that banks carried relics. Bounty duels only (§6.1). | Kill Gold & Gems, **Relics** (no artifacts, §5.4) |

### 3.3 Dynamic Tower Room Architecture & Biomes

Tower floors are dynamically managed by `TowerFloorService.luau` and generated via `TowerRoomBuilder.luau` in conjunction with `TowerLayout.luau` and `TowerBiomeAssets.luau`:
- **Room Origin Spacing Formula:**
  $$\text{Origin}(id) = \Big(12000 + ((id - 1) \bmod 10) \times 2400,\; 1000,\; 12000 + \lfloor(id - 1) / 10\rfloor \times 2400\Big)$$
  Spaced at $2,400\text{ studs}$ along both axes so enlarged, multi-tiered rooms remain completely isolated from adjacent cameras, raycasts, and simulation heartbeats.
- **Progressive 5-Floor Scale & Variant Progression:**
  Each biome spans 5 floors (`IndexInBiome = 1..5`). Rather than a static flat box, each room scales progressively across 5 layout variants guided by reference concepts:

| Floor in Biome | Variant Kind | Footprint ($W \times D \times H$) | Elevation Tiers | Architectural Theme | Mob Groups (Camps) | Total Mobs |
| :---: | :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- | :---: | :---: |
| **Floor 1** | `INTRODUCTION` | $380 \times 420 \times 130\text{ studs}$ | 3 Tiers ($Y: 0\text{ to }+12$) | Foreground courtyard, threshold entry arches, flora/crystals/dunes, north shrine. | 4 Camps + Apex | 16 live Mobs (the biome Alpha is summoned at the Apex) |
| **Floor 2** | `EXPANDED_ENCOUNTER` | $420 \times 470 \times 140\text{ studs}$ | 3 Tiers ($Y: 0\text{ to }+14$) | Flanking elevated colonnades and side galleries connected by side ramps. | 5 Camps + Apex | 22 live Mobs |
| **Floor 3** | `TERRAIN_VARIATION` | $460 \times 520 \times 155\text{ studs}$ | 4 Tiers ($Y: -4\text{ to }+18$) | Recessed elemental chasm (water/lava/mist) with arched bridge crossings and waterfalls. | 5 Camps + Apex | 25 live Mobs |
| **Floor 4** | `ELITE_STRONGHOLD` | $510 \times 580 \times 170\text{ studs}$ | 4 Tiers ($Y: -4\text{ to }+22$) | Monumental flanking guardians, watchtowers, and twin upper elite bastions. | 7 Camps + Apex | 35 live Mobs |
| **Floor 5** | `BOSS_ARENA` | $560 \times 640 \times 190\text{ studs}$ | 5 Tiers ($Y: -6\text{ to }+28$) | Full biome masterpiece: Apex Boss Dais, overhead centerpiece, and cascades. | 7 Camps + Apex | 35 live Mobs (4 Elite-led camps, placed at random) + summoned Boss |

- **Per-floor layouts (`TowerLayout.Get(index, floorId)`):** the table above gives each floor-in-biome its canonical shape; every floor then reshapes its own copy, seeded by its floor id (hand-authored floors, below, take a fixed shape instead), so no two floors are alike and none is a mirror image: galleries, bastions, the Crossing and the Apex shift, resize and change height (each side on its own), and the long climbs to the Apex often wind through a small bend terrace instead of running straight. The Sanctuary and the Basin never move. Every variation is validated (no overlapping terraces, no connector over a terrace or across another, no cramped camp) and falls back to the canonical layout if no seed passes; camps, patrols, portals, lanes and navigation all read the floor's own layout ([Tower Navigation §2a](TOWER_NAVIGATION.md)).
- **Hand-authored floors (`TowerLayout` `AUTHORED`):** a floor can replace its seeded variation with a fixed, hand-designed shape: its own terrace spots and sizes, its own path list (which terraces join which), in-world terrace names, set pieces in `TowerBiomeAssets` (keyed by `layout.Authored`), and its own lighting grade (`HubLighting.FloorGrades`, layered over the biome's). Terrace ids keep their roles (Basin = entry level, Galleries = wings, Crossing = central spine, Apex = summit), so camps, patrols, portals and navigation need no changes; authored floors pass the same `Validate` checks. The Verdant Ruins plan for all five floors is `docs/prompts/verdant-ruins-floor-layouts-prompt.md`; all five floors (1–5) are built:
  - **Floor 1, Overgrown Threshold:** the front door of the Solmere, the vanished sun-keepers whose temples the forest took (motif: the carved sun disc; the Heartroot's emerald-gold sap is what still glows). From the Sanctuary (*Fallen Gate*) the arrival ramp drops into the *Pilgrim Hollow* (the Basin), and a narrow raised *Processional Avenue* (the Crossing, 52 × 96 at Y +3, lined with sun-disc stelae) runs straight north up grand steps to the *Dawn Gate* (the Apex, Y +12): a cracked sun-gate whose surviving half frames the boss portals, its sun disc hanging askew, the other half fallen on the terrace. Two glades flank the avenue at different heights: the *Shrine Glade* (west, Y +4, a row of small sun shrines) climbs to the Dawn Gate on its own; the *Banyan Well* (east, Y +6, a Solmere well under a banyan) has no path up and rejoins the avenue instead. Six giant Heartroot figs (trunks 15 studs thick, about 60 tall) stand off the walkways, a pair over the arrival, one either side of the avenue and a pair framing the Dawn Gate, and their boughs arch over the paths with leaf canopies wrapped round every bough end and the trunk's top (the banyan's foliage only, never a whole tree stood on a branch), so the climb runs under a green canopy; boughs and hanging aerial roots stay above 38 studs, clear of every lane and camp, and the floor's ecosystem grows around them. Following the walkable-earth design philosophy, all six grassy terraces, landings, and ramps walk directly on sculpted `Terrain` (`Ground` trails, 3D `Grass`, `LeafyGrass`, and staggered `Limestone` stepping stones with non-colliding hidden proxy parts), while only the `Crossing -> Apex` `GrandStairTread`s stay solid `Limestone` masonry. Camps are unchanged (Hollow west and east, both glades, the Alpha at the summit; 16 mobs, 1 Elite). Lighting is dawn: low warm gold, cool shade and morning haze.
  - **Floor 2, Broken Aqueduct:** an asymmetric diagonal ruins layout (`verdant:2`) where the limestone viaduct (`Aqueduct South` -> `Aqueduct North`) has snapped mid-span above the reed pond — forcing a climb east around `Mill Ruin` (the waterwheel pool) and back across `Aqueduct North`, or up `Cistern Plaza`'s west stairs (`Kind = "STAIRS"`), to the off-centre north-west `Wellhead Shrine` summit. Camps hold 22 live mobs and 2 Elites across five live camps (`Mill Pond` west/east, `Aqueduct South`, `Cistern Plaza`, `Mill Ruin`) plus the `Wellhead Shrine` Alpha (`APEX_SANCTUM`). All grassy terraces (`Sanctuary`, `Mill Pond`, `Cistern Plaza`, `Mill Ruin`, `Wellhead Shrine`), grassy landing pads, and dirt-and-grass ramps render and collide directly via sculpted voxel `Terrain` (`Ground` trails + 3D `Grass` blades + staggered `Limestone` stepping stones, with hidden grassy `Part` colliders disabled so actors never float over terrain contours) and real voxel `Water` pools (no flat blue water plates), while `Aqueduct South`, `Aqueduct North`, and the west stairs stay solid Solmere limestone. Landmarks include twin snapped `BrokenAqueductSpan`s with waterfalls, the eastern `Waterwheel` in its mill-race pool, `SolmereBalustrade`s, `ReedPondCluster`s with stepping stones, four `HeartrootGiant`s, and the stepped golden `Temple` + `SunDiscShrine` in a carved mountain alcove under bright morning haze (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[2]`), with optional Blender meshes in `TowerBiomeKit.ExtraTemplates` (`assets-source/tower/verdant_f2_kit/VerdantF2Kit.fbx`).
  - **Floor 3, Rootway Crossing:** an east–west river gorge (`verdant:3`, `Bottom = -4`, chasm catch at `Y = -8`) splitting the grassy south river bank (*Fern Bank*, `Y 0`) from the sunlit north cliff summit (*Root-Throne*, `Y +18`) where a colossal Heartroot Mother-Trunk wraps the carved Solmere sun-disc throne. Three suspended Heartroot bridges at distinct heights cross the flowing voxel `Water` gorge (`liquidLevel = -4.8`): the central root-tangle island (*The Knot*, `Y +5`, climbing by root-framed limestone `STAIRS` to the throne), the west root arch (*Root Perch*, `Y +10`, overlooking a 38-stud west canyon waterfall and sculpted `RootBridgeArch`), and the east felled colossus stump crown (*Hollow Stump*, `Y +6`, concentric heartwood rings framed by `HollowStumpRim`). Two direct-`Terrain` return ramps (`ChasmWest` / `ChasmEast`) climb from the gorge riverbed shore back up to *Fern Bank*. Camps hold 25 live mobs across five camps (`Fern Bank` west/east, `The Knot`, `Root Perch`, `Hollow Stump` with 3 Elites above entry) plus the `Root-Throne` Alpha (`APEX_SANCTUM`), under noon light shafts (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[3]`) and optional Blender meshes (`assets-source/tower/verdant_f3_kit/VerdantF3Kit.fbx`).
  - **Floor 4, Vinebound Bastion:** a walled compound (`verdant:4`, `Bottom = -4`, `510 × 580`) where a still green voxel `Water` marsh moat (`liquidLevel = -4.8`) and flanking marsh channels separate the grassy direct-`Terrain` *Outer Ward* (`0, 0, 160`, `440 × 114`) from six solid weathered limestone fortress terraces (`Gatehouse` at `Y +5`, `Barracks` and `Sun Shrine` wing decks at `Y +8` joined by wall-walk bridges that unlock the `WALL_WALK` patrol route, upright `West Tower` `Y +14` and leaning `East Tower` `Y +16`, and processional stairs up through `GatehouseArch` to the `Inner Keep` summit at `Y +22`). Two direct-`Terrain` return ramps (`ChasmWest` / `ChasmEast`, `Z = 59..102`) climb from the moat back up to *Outer Ward*. Camps hold 35 live mobs across 7 camps (4 Elites) plus the `Inner Keep` Alpha (`APEX_SANCTUM`). Landmarks include the strangler-fig `GatehouseArch`, flanking `CurtainWallRuins`, twin `VineboundGuardian`s, upright and leaning `VineboundTower`s slung with high `AirborneVineCable`s toward the `VineboundKeep` backdrop, `BarracksSpearRack` & `SunShrineAltar` with roofless ruin gables, `MoatReedRaft`s, `ToppledWallRubble`, and four `HeartrootGiant`s under humid overcast afternoon haze (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[4]`), with optional Blender meshes in `TowerBiomeKit.ExtraTemplates` (`assets-source/tower/verdant_f4_kit/VerdantF4Kit.fbx`).
  - **Floor 5, Mossbound Court:** the Verdant Ruins Boss arena (`verdant:5`, `Bottom = -6`, `560 × 640`), built around a deep sunken voxel `Water` cenote sinkhole (`liquidLevel = -2.8`). Players descend from `Fallen Gate` across direct-`Terrain` *Cenote Rim* (`0, 0, 176`, `480 × 108`) and climb either the flanking two-tier stone colonnade spiral (`West Terrace` `Y +6` -> `Northwest Tier` `Y +14`, or `East Terrace` `Y +8` -> `Northeast Tier` `Y +16`) or directly across the suspended *Sunbridge* causeway (`0, 5, 40`) and root-wrapped ceremonial `STAIRS` up to the `140 × 110` *Mossbound Dais* (`Y +28`), topped with carved Solmere sun-disc floor medallions. Two direct-`Terrain` return ramps (`ChasmWest` / `ChasmEast`, `Z = 78..121`) climb out of the cenote shore. Camps hold 35 live mobs across 7 camps (4 Elites) plus the Biome Boss (`APEX_SANCTUM`, `95`-stud leash) framed by the open semicircular `CenoteColonnadeDome`, soaring `HeartrootMotherHeart` with glowing sap heart & golden-hour dusk light shafts (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[5]`), four two-tier `SolmereColonnadeWing`s, four `CenoteCascadeSpillway` waterfalls pouring into the cenote, and open `GlowLilyCluster`s, with optional Blender meshes in `TowerBiomeKit.ExtraTemplates` (`assets-source/tower/verdant_f5_kit/VerdantF5Kit.fbx`).
  - **Floor 6, Sporelit Entry (`fungal:6`):** the edge of the Veinweb, the single fungal organism that runs through all of Fungal Hollow (its cyan-violet veins are its nerves, the giant mushrooms its fruit, the Spore Matriarch its heart), in the outer quarry of the Velmari, a vanished order of lamp-masons who farmed light underground (indigo slate, violet cobble, teal crystal inlays; motif: the gill-wheel, a disc of radiating gill lines round a hanging bulb). A still teal glowpool fills the middle of the floor, and the *Elder Cap*, one colossal mushroom about 110 studs tall (violet dome, cyan gills, sixteen hanging spore-bulb tendrils), rises from a rock island in it and hides the Apex: from the Sanctuary (*Lampwright's Landing*) the player sees the cyan shaft of the cavern's oculus glowing over the cap. Two routes wrap round the pool and none crosses it (no Basin -> Crossing or Basin -> Apex path): west from the *Glowpool Shore* (the Basin) to the *Chanters' Terrace* (Y +8, raised over the pool, the Sporecap Chanters' candle altar, the Elite camp) and up the long Mycelium Ramp; or east to the *Skitter Warren* (Y +4, egg-sacs against the cliff), up the *Mason's Stair* (`Stairs`) onto the *Veinspan* (the Crossing: a 3-stud slate bridge deck on arched piers over the east pool, Y +9) and down its slate steps to the *Skylight Dais* (the Apex, Y +12) under a round oculus in an otherwise closed vault that casts shadows, so daylight only falls through the oculus, past a fringe of dangling roots, with a crown of giant caps on the cliff above. The terrain is the walking surface (`Terrain.Direct`, below); the floor's ground is violet cobble trails over an indigo spore-moss carpet, carpeted with glowcap turf and spore-moss (the biome's grass), with veins on the cliffs, teal crystal spires in the pool and by the Lantern Spores' camp, glowing pad-caps on the water and falls from the north-west and north-east cliffs. Camps are the index-1 roster (Shore west and east, both wings, the Mycelial Brute Alpha on the dais; 16 mobs, 1 Elite); the Veinspan has none. Lighting is underground twilight (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[6]`): indigo ambient, cyan from above, violet from below, a violet haze and stronger bloom. Kit meshes come from `fungal_f6_kit` (`TowerBiomeKit.ExtraTemplates`; each has a part-built fallback until imported).
  - **Floor 7, Mycelium Galleries (`fungal:7`):** the Velmari's lamp-galleries, cut in tiers into the cavern wall and now strung with the Veinweb's nets. The main route is a switchback up the wall: from the *Drip Hall* (the Basin, Y 0, roots dangling from the vault, drip puddles below) east to the *Lower Lamp Gallery* (Y +4, its lamp niches still burning, a Chanters' candle altar, Elite camp), back west across the *Netted Isle* (the Crossing, Y +8: a rock island rising out of the span pool, its top a rounded squircle (`Terrain.Shapes`) whose corners fall away into the water, with nets strung on mycelium stalks standing in the pool round it, shelf-fungus clumps on its shore and a small oculus overhead whose rim drips glowing threads), up the broad gallery steps (`Stairs`) to the *Upper Lamp Gallery* (Y +12, its lamps mostly dead, Elite camp) and east again to the *Lantern Vault* (the Apex, Y +16: dead lamp-posts round the dais and one living, colossal lamp). A shortcut ramp climbs from the Drip Hall straight onto the isle, and the long east climb from the lower gallery skips the upper one. Camps are the index-2 roster (Hall west and east 4 each, the isle 4, both galleries 5 with Elites, the Alpha in the vault; 22 mobs, 2 Elites). Lighting is deeper and more violet than Floor 6 (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[7]`), the veins thicker on the cliffs (`CliffVeins = 40`). New kit pieces (`fungal_f7_kit`): `MyceliumNet` and `LampNiche`.
  - **Floor 8, Blooming Chasm (`fungal:8`):** the cavern floor fell away and the Veinweb bloomed in the chasm; this is where the **Lampkeepers** (the last Velmari who refused the Veinweb, fleeing up toward the surface) made their refuge, and their trail runs on through Floors 9 and 10. The Sanctuary is *Lampkeepers' Rest*, their last lit camp (two cap-roofed shelters, a lit crystal brazier, a supply cache). From the *Rim of Blooms* (the Basin, Y 0, their abandoned forward camp at its west end) their **rope walkways** (`Terrain.Bridges`: plank decks with rope rails on posts, hung over the open chasm) reach the *Violet Cap* (Y +8: the walkable top of a colossal mushroom, a round deck (`Terrain.RoundDecks`) with a curled lip, glowing gills and a stem down into its pool; Elite camp) and the *Crystal Islet* (Y +4, terrain, teal spires; Elite camp). Both reach the *Old Aqueduct* (the Crossing, Y +12: a surviving Velmari span running east-west on arched piers, the broken spans' last piers standing in the chasm under the walkways, the Lampkeepers' barricaded watch-post at its north-west corner; Elite camp), whose stairs climb to the *Bloomcrown Ledge* (the Apex, Y +20); the islet also climbs the Spine, a rock ridge, straight to the ledge. Their chalk sign (a gill-wheel with one long ray pointing up) marks the walkway heads and the ledge, and a crushed camp lies on the chasm floor below the islet for those who fall. The chasm floor carries teal pools and a sea of mid-size caps, under the widest oculus yet; lighting is the brightest Fungal grade (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[8]`), the Rest's brazier the one warm pool of light. Camps are the index-3 roster (25 mobs, 3 Elites); the Violet Cap's patrol walks its walkway to the aqueduct. New kit pieces (`fungal_f8_kit`): `CapShelter`, `CampSupplies`, `CrystalBrazier`, `Barricade`, `ChalkSign`.
  - **Floor 9, Rotcap Keep (`fungal:9`):** the Velmari citadel devoured from the inside by rotcaps, and the scene of the **Lampkeepers' last stand**. The layout is an **"H" with a high keystone** over the central rot pit: from *The Broken Gate* (the Sanctuary, Y +8, torn shelters, shattered barricades, tattered banners) and the *Fallen Quarry* (the Basin, Y 0, Brute club-drag scars, toppled blocks, second breached barricade line), routes split up the flanking wings — west through the *Brewing Cellars* (`WestGallery`, Y +8, bubbling rot vats, the Lampkeepers' overrun camp turned into a sickly green Chanter candle shrine) to the *West Rot-Spire* (`WestBastion`, Y +16, split watchtower; Elite camp), and east through the *Ossuary of Caps* (`EastGallery`, Y +12, giant ribs and skulls sprouted with rotcaps, dead lamps among the bones) to the *East Rot-Spire* (`EastBastion`, Y +20, leaning tower bound in mycelium, the Lampkeepers' last `ChalkSign` pointing north; Elite camp). High stone-and-plank spans (`Terrain.Bridges`, arched piers in the chasm) cross from both rot-spires to *The Keystone* (`Crossing`, Y +20: masonry deck on arched piers above the rot pit, breached barricade at its north-west corner; `BASTION_RING` patrol walks across it), whose stairs climb to the roofless *Keep of the Rotcap* (`Apex`, Y +24: flanking keep towers and ruined curtain walls crowned by a colossal rotcap rising atop a keep shelf above the portal lanes). **No warm Flamekeepers' light anywhere on the floor**; lighting shifts to sickly yellow-green mixed into the violet (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[9]`), with yellow-green oculus spotlight, alternating yellow-green and violet `VaultLantern`s and set-piece `PointLight`s (24 lights total), and a 50/50 yellow-green and violet ecosystem mushroom mix (`TowerBiomeAssets`). Camps are the index-4 roster (35 live mobs, 4 Elites). New kit pieces (`fungal_f9_kit`): `BrokenBarricade`, `RotVat`, `OssuaryBones`, `RotcapTower`, `VelmariBanner`, `RotcapCluster`, `RotMyceliumWeb`.
  - **Floor 10, Matriarch's Hollow (`fungal:10`):** the heart of the Veinweb and the end of the Lampkeepers' trail. The Matriarch's Dais (`Apex`, 0, 28, −60, 140 × 110, a round terrain mesa, `Shapes`) stands in the middle of the cavern in a dark moat under the great oculus (`Skylight`, radius 70), with four silk-wrapped `ShroudSpire`s in the water at its corners, so the boss arena is in plain sight from the arrival but out of reach. The floor is the walk round to the one way in: from *The Last Lamp* (the Sanctuary: `LastLampShrine`, the floor's only warm light, a `CapShelter`) and the *Silk Shore* (the Basin, Y 0: `SilkWebCurtain`s, brood clutches and crystals, chalk signs), rim paths climb to the lower ring, the *West Egg Gallery* (Y +8: `EggRack`s either side of a `WebbedArchway`, a cold Chanter altar) and the *Spinneret Ledge* (Y +12: `SilkWindlass`es, loom cables down into the moat); wall stairs (`Stairs`) climb to the upper ring, the *West* and *East Cocoon Arcades* (`WestBastion`/`EastBastion`, Y +16/+20: a `SilkSpire`, a curtain and an `EggRack` each); hung silk spans (`Terrain.Bridges`) cross from both arcades to the *Brood Pillar* (`Crossing`, 0, 12, −262: a deck over the brood pool on a fungus-and-silk column, `BroodEggClutch`es and `SilkSpool`s) at the back of the nest; and the *Silk Stair* runs south from there across the moat to the dais' north rim (two `CocoonLamp`s at its head), its only link. The boss portals stand on the dais' south rim (`Portals = {Side = "SOUTH"}`), either side of the Veinweb heart (an `ElderCap` growing out of the dais floor, its roots in a mound of mossy slate with silk veins running out across the dais, framed by two spires), with the stolen lamps (`StolenLampCocoon`) round the rim outside the telegraph discs. No apex shelf rises behind the dais (`Terrain.NoApexShelf`). Camps are the index-5 roster (35 mobs, 4 Elites, the Spore Matriarch on the dais). Lighting (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[10]`) is the clearest Fungal grade, pale silk-white air with cyan from the oculus and a magenta bounce, the opposite of Floor 9's murk. Kit meshes come from `fungal_f10_kit` and `fungal_f10_nest_kit` (which adds the `SILK` colour role).
  - **Floor 11, Cinder Gate (`ashen:1`):** the Varrash frontier threshold (`PathStyle = "VARRASH"`, `DirectTerrain = true`, `Liquid = { Dry = true }`), designed around direct-`Terrain` volcanic ash/sand plains (`Ash Flats`, `0, 0, 82`), wind-carved sandstone ridges (including the central *Sleeping Dune* crest sweeping north behind the dead-end *Dune Crest* lookout at `(0, 10, -20)` topped with `DuneWaystationRuins`), western *Toll House* (`-116, 4, -20`), eastern *Watch Kiln* (`118, 7, -44`), and northern *Cinder Gate* summit (`0, 12, -150`). Camps hold 16 live mobs (1 Elite on *Toll House*; no camp on *Dune Crest*) under searing desert noon haze (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[11]`). Set pieces mirror `AshenF11Kit.fbx` (`assets-source/tower/ashen_f11_kit/AshenF11Kit.fbx`, with native fallbacks): `SanctuaryVarrashSteps`, `VarrashWaystone` caravan markers, tilted `BuriedWagon`, `BrokenMidArch`, `TiltedSandstoneMonolith`s, banded `TollHouseRuins` + nomad tents, cylindrical `WatchKilnTower`, monumental `CinderGateArch` set into columnar basalt cliffs, glowing ember crystal patches, and sun-bleached dead snags. Authored floors configure `DirectTerrain`, `Decks`, `Suspended`, `Masonry`, `Liquid`, `TerrainFeatures`, and custom `Patrols` directly inside `TowerLayout.AUTHORED`.
  - **Floor 12, Buried Caravan (`ashen:2`):** an east-west-east single switchback caravan road (`PathStyle = "VARRASH"`, `DirectTerrain = true`, `Decks = { Crossing = true }`, `Masonry = "VARRASH_DECK"`, `Liquid = { Dry = true }`) climbing from the *Wagon Circle* entry (`0, 0, 110`) framed by `DesertCaravanWagon`s and a nomad campfire ring, up through the colossal arching `SandStriderRibs` straddling `EastGallery -> Crossing` (`Strider's Ribs`, `130, 5, 10`, with `SandStriderSkull` beside it), across the solid weathered-sandstone *Caravanserai Roof* deck (`-10, 9, -30`) topped with flush bronze `CaravanseraiSkylight` grates, past collapsed `SpiceVaultRuins` spilling saffron and crimson powder (`-130, 12, -80`), up to the off-centre tented `MasterTombPavilion` (`40, 14, -170`) under diffuse amber afternoon sandstorm dust (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[12]`) and swirling glasswind dust devils (`assets-source/tower/ashen_f12_kit/AshenF12Kit.fbx`). Camps hold 22 live mobs (2 Elites on *Spice Vault* and *Strider's Ribs*).
  - **Floor 13, Glasswind Ravine (`ashen:3`):** a north–south obsidian canyon chasm (`PathStyle = "VARRASH"`, `DirectTerrain = true`, `Bottom = -4`, `Decks = { Crossing = true }`, `Suspended = { Platforms = { Crossing = true }, Links = { WestGallery_Crossing = true, EastGallery_Crossing = true, Crossing_Apex = true } }`, `Masonry = "VARRASH_GLASS"`, `Liquid = { Dry = true }`, `TerrainFeatures = { RAVINE, DUNE }`) dividing high *West Ledge* (`-140, 10, -34`) from lower *East Ledge* (`140, 6, -56`); the east ledge reaches *Lightning Scar* (`0, 18, -185`) only by crossing the suspended obsidian-and-amber *Glass Arch* (`0, 5, -78`) and climbing black-glass stairs with amber inlay (`Crossing_Apex`), with authored `WEST_LEDGE` and `ARCH_CROSSING` patrols replacing generic gallery routes and two ravine return ramps back to *Ravine Mouth* (`0, 0, 124`). Landmarks mirror `AshenF13Kit.fbx` (`assets-source/tower/ashen_f13_kit/AshenF13Kit.fbx`, with native fallbacks): colossal diagonal `GlassArchSpan`, crystalline `FulguriteCluster` organ pipes and glittering chasm shards, distant NE `RavineHorizonSpire`, NW `RavineSandfall` cascade, overhanging west cliff glass eaves, right-bank Ash Raider camp tents, `GlassCactusCluster` blooms, and twin `VarrashLightningRod`s framing *Lightning Scar* under ravine dusk lighting (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[13]`). Camps hold 25 live mobs (3 Elites on *West Ledge*, *East Ledge*, and *Glass Arch*).
  - **Floor 14, Sunscar Fortress (`ashen:4`):** an Elite stronghold (`510 × 580 × 170`, `PathStyle = "VARRASH"`, `DirectTerrain = true`, `Bottom = -4`, `Masonry = "VARRASH_FORTRESS"`, `Decks` & `Suspended` across `Crossing`, `WestGallery`, `EastGallery`, `WestBastion`, `EastBastion`, and `Apex`, `Liquid = { LevelOffset = 3.2, FloorDip = 1.6 }`, `TerrainFeatures = { LAVA_CHANNEL }`) split down the center (`X = 0`) by the `CrackedLava` **Sunscar** magma fissure and `SunscarLavaSeam` glow veins. Players climb from the direct-`Terrain` *Ashmarket* courtyard (`0, 0, 150`, dressed with `AshmarketStall`s, dry ash fountain, and amber glass shards) solely through the `PortcullisGate` barbican chokepoint at *Kiln Gate* (`0, 5, 52`), cross between *Glassblowers' Row* (`-160, 8, 0`) and *Cistern Barracks* (`160, 8, -10`) over the suspended *Scar Bridge* (`WestGallery_EastGallery`, supported by basalt piers outside the lava trench, adding the authored `SCAR_BRIDGE` patrol alongside generic gallery/bastion routes), and ascend past *West Kiln* (`KilnTower`, `-160, 14, -150`) and *Signal Tower* (`SignalLensTower`, `160, 16, -160`) to the glass-floored *Sunscar Throne* (`0, 22, -205`, `SunscarThroneGlassFloor` over `EmberVeinTapCore`, `SunscarThroneKeep`), lit by readable night ambient + kiln/lava bloom (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[14]`, `assets-source/tower/ashen_f14_kit/AshenF14Kit.fbx`). Camps hold 35 live mobs (4 Elites).
  - **Floor 15, Colossus Dais (`ashen:5`):** the Ashen Dunes Biome Boss arena (`560 × 640 × 190`, `PathStyle = "VARRASH"`, `DirectTerrain = true`, `Bottom = -6`, `Masonry = "VARRASH_DAIS"`, `Decks` & `Suspended` on `Crossing` & `Apex` plus suspended `WestGallery_Crossing`, `Crossing_WestBastion`, `Crossing_Apex`, `WestBastion_Apex`, and `EastBastion_Apex`, `Liquid = { LevelOffset = 3.2, FloorDip = 1.6 }`, `TerrainFeatures = { CENOTE_LAGOON }` rendered as a cracked-lava caldera lake with `CalderaLavaSeams`). Players ascend from *Caldera Floor* (`0, 0, 176`, with Ash Raider outpost tents and two chasm return ramps) across the body of a fallen Elder Colossus — over the curled stone *Fallen Hand* (`-180, 6, 30`, `ElderHandFingers`), along the arm-guarded forearm bridge onto the heart-caged *Elder's Chest* (`-30, 10, -40`), up the upper arm to *Shoulder* (`-150, 16, -150`, `ElderShoulderPauldron` ringed by basalt columns) — or scale *Broken Shield* (`180, 8, 20`, `ElderShieldRim`) and *Fallen Crown* (`170, 14, -140`, `ElderCrownRing` & face-down `ElderColossusHead`), or climb the exposed trade-road `STAIRS` (`Crossing_Apex`) straight up to the `140 × 110` *Colossus Dais* (`0, 28, -232`, `APEX_SANCTUM` Biome Boss Sandstone Colossus, `95`-stud leash) framed by basalt columns and the erupting `CinderholdVent` caldera behind the dais under crimson volcanic eruption lighting (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[15]`, `assets-source/tower/ashen_f15_kit/AshenF15Kit.fbx`). Authored patrols (`FOREARM`, `SHIELD_CLIMB`, `CROWN_RING`) replace generic gallery routes alongside `BASIN_LOOP`, with 35 live camp mobs (4 Elites) plus the Boss.
  - **Floor 21, Slagworks Entry (`infernal:1`):** the outer works of the Sootwrights, the smith-artificers who hollowed the mountain over the root of the Ember Vein; their sigil is the hammer-ring (a hammer inside a ring of gear teeth). The first floor built to the **escalation ladder** (`docs/prompts/infernal-foundry-floor-layouts-prompt.md` Part 2, the standing rule for Floors 21–50: every floor bigger, richer and more varied than the one below it and than every earlier floor at its position). It overrides its room size (`Size = { Width = 480, Depth = 525, Height = 160 }`, 1.58× Floor 1's footprint) and has 9 terraces, 9 links and 8 walk heights, against 8 / 8 / 6 at best on Floors 1, 6, 11 and 16; the walk from the Sanctuary to the Apex stays within 1.5× the canonical floor's. **Shape: a hub with spokes.** From the *Slagworks Gate* (Sanctuary) the *Ore Yard* (Basin, terrain, Y 0) crosses the *Weighbridge* (an `ExtraPlatforms` deck, Y 2) onto the *Great Turntable* (Crossing, a diamond-plate deck, Y 4), which every route passes. Spurs run from it to the *Sorting Shed* (WestGallery, Y 8, Elite camp), the *Slag Tip* (EastGallery, Y 12), the *Ore Crusher* (a deck on an iron gantry, Y 16, reached by two girder bridges) and the *Coal Bunker* (a deck, Y 6); the iron *Furnace Steps* (16 treads with brass nosing) climb to the *Furnace Gate* (Apex, Y 20), and a rim track joins the Sorting Shed to the Ore Crusher. `PathStyle = "SOOTWRIGHT"` (sleepers and steel rails down every earthen ramp), `Masonry = "FOUNDRY_IRON"` (riveted decks on soot-brick plinths, girder spurs on trestles, iron handrails), `Surface = "SOOTWRIGHT"` (sooty basalt yards, slate-grey tracks and ash patches; never `Brick` or `WoodPlanks`, which Roblox collides as 4-stud blocks), and a new `SLAG_TIP` terrain feature heaps clinker with glowing seams beside the east spur and in the back corners. Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/InfernalFoundry.luau`, footprints checked against every lane and camp by the specs): a tank engine and ore tub parked on the turntable with a cab hut on its rim, the weighbridge office, the jaw crusher with twin flywheels, coal hoppers over the bunker's siding, a slag ladle tipping over the Slag Tip, the open Sorting Shed with roof chutes, a water tower, a roasting kiln, sidings with tubs and buffer stops, a derailed tub, the hammer-ring Furnace Gate in a soot-brick works with window glows and ducts, six smoking flue stacks (up to 120 studs), the bellows duct on trestles and a lava fall in the far corner, plus work clutter on every terrace and at both ends of every link and coal, ore and clinker ground cover. The kit (`tools/blender/generate_infernal_f21_kit.py`, 12 templates in `ExtraTemplates`, each with a part-built fallback) and a smoky red dawn grade (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[21]`).
  - **Floor 22, Molten Assembly (`infernal:2`):** the Sootwrights' assembly hall under a closed brick vault (`Skylight` oculus over the Inspection Gallery), 550 × 615 × 180, 11 terraces, 12 links and 10 walk heights (above Floor 21 and Floors 2, 7, 12 and 17). **Shape: a gantry "Π" with a stem.** A gantry crane spans the hall on two lattice piers and its girder is the route: *West Gantry Pier* (Y 8) → *West Hoist* (Y 9) → *Crane Trolley* (Crossing, Y 10) → *East Hoist* (Y 11) → *East Gantry Pier* (Y 12), tilted by a failed jack. Its four spans are `GirderSpans` (deep lattice box girders with no trestles; TowerRoomBuilder). From the trolley a stem climbs over the *Inspection Gallery* (Y 18) and up an iron stair to the *Assembly Hall* (Apex, Y 24, its only way in); each pier carries a dead-end loft (*Pattern Loft*, *Control Cab*), and a loading ramp from the *Assembly Yard* (Basin) to the trolley keeps the walk to the hall within 1.5× the canonical floor's. Lava runnels (`LAVA_CHANNEL`) run down both side aisles. Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/InfernalFoundry.luau`): hoist portals with half-built Forge Constructs hanging on chain hoists beside the girder walk, constructs in assembly scaffolds on the yard floor, the trolley's winch drums and hook under its deck, an overhead travelling crane on wall-pier runways with its hook lowered over a bay, the master jig in the Assembly Hall (a construct 81 studs tall in a 96-stud scaffold) between two brick furnaces venting steam, great gears, an idle gantry beyond, steaming quench tanks, roller conveyors, wooden patterns in the Pattern Loft, the glazed Control Cab, parts racks, the failed jack under the east pier, brick vault ribs and sodium lamp strings. Kit: `tools/blender/generate_infernal_f22_kit.py` (12 templates); grade: warm sodium work-light (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[22]`).
  - **Floor 23, Furnace Causeway (`infernal:3`):** the furnace line over a lava lake (625 × 700 × 205, `Bottom = -4`, a `CENOTE_LAGOON` of cracked lava with crust ridges; return ramps climb from the lava shore). 12 terraces, 12 links and 11 walk heights (above Floor 22 and Floors 3, 8, 13 and 18). **Shape: a fishbone.** The spine is a three-span causeway on soot-brick piers (`StonePiers`: piers with basalt cutwaters, a rail line up every deck and twin pipe runs along the spans): *South Causeway* (Crossing, Y 6) → *Mid Causeway* (Y 12) → *North Causeway* (Y 20), then an iron stair to the *Furnace Mouth* (Apex, Y 28). Six ribs leave it, two per span on alternate sides: *West Smelting Bay* (Elite camp) and *Slag Bay*; *East Quench Bay* (Elite camp) and *Cooling Bay*; *Casting Bay* and *Blade Gallery*. An iron catwalk joins the Quench Bay up to the Blade Gallery. Authored patrols replace the generic ones (`SPINE`, `CATWALK`, plus the Basin loop). Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/InfernalFoundry.luau`): six furnace towers on plinths in the lava flanking the spine, mouths toward the causeway, fed by bellows ducts; the tilting crucible pouring into a grid of mould beds under a jib crane in the Casting Bay; a slag ladle tipping off the Slag Bay into the lava; steaming quench tanks and steam vents; racks of glowing blades in the Cooling Bay; weapon displays in the Blade Gallery; the giant furnace door at the Mouth between flue stacks (115 studs) with more stacks along the back wall; coal tubs, sidings and jib cranes on the *Charging Floor*; crust floes adrift on the lake. Kit: `tools/blender/generate_infernal_f23_kit.py` (11 templates); grade: furnace glare (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[23]`).
  - **Floor 24, Ironflame Bulwark (`infernal:4`):** the Foundry's defences inside a volcanic cavern (715 × 810 × 230, `Bottom = -4`; two north–south `LAVA_CHANNEL` trenches split the fortress into three fortified strips, with `POUR_PIT` carving the boiling **Pour** magma cauldron south of the gate and six `LAVA_FALL` cascades spilling down the outer cavern walls; return ramps climb from the lava shore). 14 terraces, 14 links and 12 walk heights (above Floor 23 and Floors 4, 9, 14 and 19; camps hold 35 live mobs with 4 raised Elites). **Shape: a weave across the strips.** The *Mustering Yard* (Basin, terrain, Y 0) links **only** to the *West Gun Deck* (WestGallery, Y 8) via a suspended iron bridge. From there squads either climb the west strip (*West Gun Deck* → iron stair to *Powder Store* → iron bridge to *West Bulwark Tower* → iron bridge to *Ironflame Citadel*, with the dead-end *Signal Bastion* beyond the tower) or weave east over suspended girder bridges through both lava-channel *Sluice Towers* (`WestSluice`, `EastSluice`, on `StonePiers`) and the *Ironflame Gate* (Crossing, Y 14, with the dead-end *Ironflame Forge* sealed north of it) onto the *East Foundry Deck* → iron stair to *Hammer Hall* → iron bridge to *East Bulwark Tower* → iron bridge to *Ironflame Citadel* (Apex, terrain, Y 36). Authored `WEST_STRIP` and `WEAVE` patrols run alongside `BASIN_LOOP` and `BASTION_RING`. Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/InfernalFloor24.luau`, footprints spec-checked against every lane and camp): iron-bound crenellated `BulwarkWall`s along both channels, twin `SluiceTower`s with `ChainWinch`es and spiked `SluiceGateChain` portcullis grates barring the lava canals, the Pour crucible chute and boiling glow seams, `SlagCannon` batteries on the Gun Deck, `PowderKegStack`s and a `JibCrane` munitions hoist in the Powder Store, the roaring `SignalBrazier` beacon and 136-stud flaming watchtower on the Signal Bastion, blazing `FlameJetPortcullis` archways at the Ironflame Gatehouse and sealed Ironflame Forge, colossal steam `DropHammer`s and hanging chains under a vaulted arch in the Hammer Hall, `SiegeBallista`s on the battlements, garrison `ArmourRack`s, ruined `Barricade`s ringing the Pour in the Mustering Yard, the 141.8-stud monumental `CitadelDoors` keep with the embossed hammer-ring sigil flanked by turret braziers and 135-stud flue stacks, and six glowing molten lava falls down the dark cavern cliffs. Kit: `tools/blender/generate_infernal_f24_kit.py` (12 templates); grade: siege-red night with cool steel-blue shadow fill (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[24]`).
  - **Floor 25, Titan's Crucible (`infernal:5`):** the Infernal Foundry Biome Boss arena (`740 × 795 × 250`, `Bottom = -6`, a massive `CENOTE_LAGOON` boiling lava lake with crust `PRESSURE_RIDGE`s and six towering wall `LAVA_FALL` cascades; return ramps climb from the lava shore). 15 terraces, 16 links and 13 walk heights (the grandest floor in the tower so far, strictly beating Floor 24 and Floors 5, 10, 15 and 20; 35 live camp mobs, 4 Elites, plus the **Ember Titan** Biome Boss summoned on the `170 × 124` dark-iron *Titan's Crucible Dais* at `Y 44`, `95`-stud leash). **Shape: an hourglass with side chambers.** Twin wide iron stairs climb from the *Casting Floor* (Basin, Y 0) to *West Mould Station* and *East Mould Station* (each with a dead-end side chamber: *Pattern Shop* and *Ingot Store*); both converge on the *Pour Gate* pinch (`Crossing`, Y 20, on `StonePiers` over the lava lake — no `Basin→Crossing` and no `Crossing→Apex`, so every route passes the pinch). North of the pinch sit the dead-end *Pour Spout* overlook (`Y 28`) and twin *West/East Duct* bridgeheads climbing to the *West/East Bellows* towers (`Y 30 / 32`), each with a high dead-end *Chain Gallery* (`Y 36 / 40`) beside the dais. Authored patrols replace generic gallery/bastion routes (`LOWER_PINCH`, `UPPER_PINCH`, `CRUCIBLE_RING` plus `BASIN_LOOP`). Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/InfernalFloor25.luau`, 39 spec-checked footprints): the colossal **179.5-stud** `GreatCrucible` on twin geared trunnion towers behind the dais (never overhanging the arena or boss portals) with the blazing `TitanHeartGrate`, cathedral `VaultRibArch` columns and `Y 270` cavern vault ribs, twin `GreatBellows` towers feeding sweeping `DuctManifold` pipes, the `PourSpoutChute` funnel catching the dais overflow waterfall, upper `ChainGalleryRail`s with overhead runway beams carrying `HangingChainRun` hooks 46+ studs above walkways, `PatternLathe`s, `TitanMouldFist` & `TitanMouldHelm` castings, stacked `IngotPallet`s, finished weapon displays, Titan tool anvils, ladle carts, crust floes, and invisible lava-lake light anchors. Kit: `tools/blender/generate_infernal_f25_kit.py` (12 templates); grade: molten gold under-light under a dark cathedral vault (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[25]`).
  - **Floor 26, Stormgrass Rise (`thunder:1`):** the Kairnfolk's lower grazing slopes, the first Thunder Highlands floor (`docs/prompts/thunder-highlands-floor-layouts-prompt.md`; guide image `reference/Thunder Highlands 26.jpg`). The Kairnfolk were storm-shepherds, cliff-masons and copper-smiths; their sigil is the forked-bolt totem (a lightning glyph bound by three copper grounding rings). `510 × 550 × 180`, open sky, no chasm; 10 terraces, 11 links and 9 walk heights (above Floor 21 and Floors 1, 6, 11 and 16), the Apex at Y 24, and the walk from the Sanctuary to the Apex 1.49× the canonical floor's. **Shape: wind-tacking contours round a sheer central headwall.** From the *Bell-Gate Fold* (Sanctuary) the trail passes under a dolmen bell-gate onto *Shepherd's Moor* (Basin, Y 0), which climbs to three staggered contours: the *Windmill Knoll* (WestGallery, Y 4, Elite camp), the *Cairn Fold* (Crossing, Y 8, no camp) and the *Standing Stones* (EastGallery, a dry-stone deck, Y 6). North of the Fold a sheer turf-capped slate headwall (`HEADWALL` terrain feature) rises some 66 studs, twin falls spilling down it into a plunge tarn: there is no `Crossing → Apex` link, so every ascent tacks out across the wind, west through the *West Wind-Crag* (Y 16) and up a copper-nosed slate stair, or east over dry-stone causeways through the *East Wind-Crag* (a deck, Y 18) to the *Stormgrass Crown* (Apex, Y 24). Two dead-end spurs overlook the moor: the *Shepherd's Watch* (a deck, Y 10) and the *Fulgurite Outcrop* (Y 12), with a scorched strike crater (`LIGHTNING_SCAR`) upslope. `PathStyle = "KAIRN"` (slate stepping flags down the stormgrass trails), `Masonry = "KAIRN_STONE"` (battered dry-stone platforms with turf lips and copper corner bands, dry-stone causeways with culverts, coped parapets and copper-banded bollards, solid slate bridgehead aprons), `Surface = "KAIRN"` (Grass, LeafyGrass, Slate trails, Rock banks, Mud tarn beds). Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/ThunderHighlands.luau`, footprints checked against every lane, camp and mob slot by the specs): the split granite tor on the headwall crowned by a copper forked-bolt totem crackling with static (the skyline's tallest piece, about 150 studs over the moor), the twin falls, a ruined post windmill on the knoll's shoulder with its sails to the gale, a ring of seven rune stones round the Standing Stones slung with glowing grounding chains, a sheepfold with its bothy, hurdles and sheep on the Cairn Fold, the shepherd's croft, prayer flags and watch cairn on the Watch, storm-glass on the Outcrop and in the crater, a storm urn shrine on the West Wind-Crag, the summit totem behind the Crown with cairns at its corners, ridge totems on the far peaks, dry-stone field walls with copper grounding stakes across the moor, wind-bent pines and stormgrass all leaning one way down the north-west gale, and moorland clutter on every terrace and at both ends of every link. Kit: `tools/blender/generate_thunder_f26_kit.py` (15 templates, each with a part-built fallback); grade: storm-breaking highland dawn (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[26]`).
  - **Floor 27, Thunderhead Outpost (`thunder:2`):** the Kairnfolk's hilltop ringfort and signal station, where the shepherds took refuge and relayed storm warnings down the mountain by wind-harp and beacon (guide image `reference/Thunder Highlands 27.jpg`). `600 × 605 × 205`, open sky; 12 terraces, 14 links and 11 walk heights (above Floor 26 and Floors 2, 7, 12, 17 and 22), the Lodge at Y 32, 22 live camp mobs and 2 Elites. **Shape: a concentric ringfort entered only through its twin palisades.** The *Stockade Apron* (Basin, Y 0) has no way straight in: it climbs only to the *West* and *East Palisade* bastions (Y 6 deck, Y 8), into the flanking *West Ram Yard* (Y 12, Elite camp) and *East Forge Yard* (a deck, Y 14, Elite camp). From the yards, routes cut in to the *Signal Broch* (Crossing, Y 16), push out to the two *Wind-Harp* towers on the ridge (Y 18 deck, Y 24), or sweep round the *Inner Ramparts* (Y 20, deck Y 22); three approaches (both inner ramparts and the *Broch Ascent* stair from the Broch) converge on *Thunderhead Lodge* (Apex, Y 32). The walk to the Lodge is 1.62× the canonical floor's: the outer arc is the floor's signature, so there is no centre bypass. The boss portals stand 44 studs either side of centre (`Portals.Spread`), so the broch keep can rise between them. Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/ThunderFloor27.luau`, using the shared `ThunderHighlands.Helpers`; fixed footprints checked by the specs): the stepped broch keep behind the Lodge with its copper cage lantern and twin forked spires (about 117 studs), dry-stone rampart rings banded in verdigris along every terrain terrace's edge (fitted round the walkways, so every path is a gap), a stone gate-arch over the arrival causeway, a palisade of copper-tipped stakes along the Apron's north edge with watch-bell gallows at its gates, the Signal Broch's smoking beacon braziers and capacitor casks, cable masts slinging braided copper out to the two wind-harps, the copper-smith's forge and wire reels in the Forge Yard, the ram pen and loading chute in the Ram Yard, longhouse ruins on the outer slopes, ridge beacons and totems, wind-bent pine shelterbelts and stormgrass. Kit: `tools/blender/generate_thunder_f27_kit.py` (13 templates, each with a part-built fallback); grade: gathering thunderhead noon (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[27]`).
  - **Floor 28, Skybridge Cliffs (`thunder:3`):** the mountain breaks into freestanding granite needles over a sea of storm cloud, spanned only by the Kairnfolk's rope-and-copper suspension skybridges (guide image `reference/Thunder Highlands 28.jpg`). `655 × 700 × 235`, open sky, `Bottom = -6` with two return ramps out of the abyss; 13 terraces, 16 links and 12 walk heights (above Floor 27 and Floors 3, 8, 13, 18 and 23), the Skybridge Crown at Y 40, 25 live camp mobs and 3 Elites (one raised, on the Central Sky-Spire at Y 20). **Shape: a staggered sky-pinnacle archipelago over a cloud abyss.** From the *Cloudbreak Ledge* (Basin, Y 0) three chains climb needles at staggered X, Z and Y (no two neighbours on one axis): a western chain (*West Sea-Stack* Y 8, *West Pinion Crag* Y 16, *West Cable Pylon* Y 28), a central chord (*Central Sky-Spire* Y 20, *Thunderstep Crag* Y 36, then the cliff-cut *Thunderstep* stair to the Crown) and an eastern chain (*East Sea-Stack* Y 12, *East Pinion Crag* Y 24, *East Cable Pylon* Y 30, a deck), cross-linked mid-gorge, with two dead-end roosts (the *Gale Roost*, Y 14, and the *Fulgurite Needle*, Y 34, both decks) over the clouds. Every link past the ledge but the Thunderstep is a hung skybridge (`Masonry = "SKYBRIDGE"`: plank deck on stringers, twin dry-stone pylons banded in copper at each bridgehead, sagging copper main cables and hemp hangers, all dressing over a straight deck). The `SEA_STACK_APRON` terrain feature makes every terrace a sheer needle and leaves the abyss floor bare slate. Authored patrols: `WEST_SKYLINE`, `EAST_SKYLINE`, `CLOUD_SPAN` beside `BASIN_LOOP`; the walk to the Crown is 1.47× the canonical floor's. Set pieces (`TowerSetPieces/ThunderFloor28.luau`; fixed footprints checked by the specs): the twin crown towers behind the Crown (145 studs), their half-arches reaching for each other with lightning arcing in the gap; a sculpted cloud sea of cloud-bank meshes in two layers (always 4+ studs under the walkways), drifting mist and violet underglow from invisible anchors; seven sea-stack spires out of the clouds (two snapped, their cables trailing) and a far range of needles with a distant hanging bridge; cable capstans on both pylons, the supply windlass on the ledge, the anemometer mast on the Sky-Spire, squall-horn perches on the pinion crags, the Storm Roc's talon nest and shed feathers on the Gale Roost, a fulgurite spire on the Fulgurite Needle, a snapped skybridge stub off the East Sea-Stack barred by cairns and rope, prayer pennants and storm lanterns at the bridgeheads, thunder-glass waystones, copper grounding chains under the high spans and cables draped down the needle faces, bonsai pines and stormgrass. Kit: `tools/blender/generate_thunder_f28_kit.py` (13 templates, each with a part-built fallback); grade: cloud-sea squall afternoon (`HubLighting.FloorGrades[28]`).
  - **Floor 29, Tempest Redoubt (`thunder:4`):** the Kairnfolk's two-tier mountain acropolis, split by an east-west cliff fault and built to channel the mountain's lightning into storm-batteries (guide image `reference/Thunder Highlands 29.jpg`). `735 × 790 × 260`, open sky, `Bottom = -6` with two return ramps out of the chasm; 15 terraces, 17 links and 13 walk heights (above Floor 28 and Floors 4, 9, 14, 19 and 24), the Tempest Redoubt at Y 48, 35 live camp mobs and 4 Elites (both Stormkeep Elites stand above the fault, at Y 30 and 32). **Shape: a two-tier acropolis on a cliff fault, three ways up it.** South of the fault (`CLIFF_FAULT` terrain: a plateau 28 studs over the chasm floor behind a sheer, stepped scarp) the lower ward stands on rock over a misty chasm: the walled causeway from the *Outer Causeway* (Basin, Y 0) into the *Lower Barbican* (Crossing, Y 8), the *West* and *East Ram Yards* (Y 12, deck Y 14) and the *Horn Posts* (Y 16) on the outer cliff edges.
