The cutscene blends over whichever camera is active (default, squad bird's-eye or duel) and never changes the field of view; shakes asked for during it shake the cutscene camera (`SkillCutscene.Shake`), since the usual combat shake would be overwritten.
  - **Several Skills at once** (a full squad often fires two together). Skills that fire within 0.1 s of each other are weighed for the camera: a hero's first Skill of the session first, then the rarest hero, then the first to fire. That one gets the cutscene; the others, and any Skill that fires while a cutscene is on screen, get a **cut-in banner** (`Rendering/SkillCutIn`): a strip in the hero's Skill colours with a live close-up of the hero (`HeroCard.Portrait`), its name and the skill's name. It slides in from the right edge under the top letterbox bar, holds about 1.25 s and slides back out; up to four stack downwards (the oldest makes way). Under Reduce Motion banners appear and disappear without sliding. Combat timing is untouched: nothing waits for a cutscene.
  - **Skips.** A Skill whose cue arrives after 40% of its cast gets no cutscene. A hero whose first Skill went to a cut-in keeps its cutscene for a later Skill. Under Reduce Motion the cutscene has no camera move: only the bars and caption show (the wind-up still plays, as part of the Skill).
- **Studio:** analytics never leave Studio. The Workspace attribute `DebugOnboardingTelemetry = true` prints every guide event and milestone to Output, and `OnboardingArmForce` (a server-side attribute) picks the arm of a new profile.

### 4.8 Free Rewards: Daily, Weekly, Playtime, Codes & Friend Boost

Opened from the **Rewards** button on the Right Rail, which shows a badge with the number of rewards ready to claim. Tunables live in `RewardsConfig`; the rules are pure (`RewardRules`) and the server (`RewardsService`) checks every claim against its own clock. **All resets are UTC:** days roll over at 00:00 UTC and weeks on Monday 00:00 UTC.

| Reward | Rule | Payout |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Daily login** | One claim per day. Claiming on consecutive days walks a 7-day calendar and then loops; a missed day restarts it at Day 1. | 2,000 Gold · 50 Gems · 5,000 Gold · 2 Promotion Stones · 100 Gems · 10,000 Gold · 300 Gems + 3 Promotion Stones |
| **Weekly chest** | One claim per week. | 30,000 Gold, 500 Gems, 5 Promotion Stones |
| **Playtime** | 7 tiers by session time played, each claimed once; claiming any unlocked tier also claims all other unlocked, unclaimed tiers. Time and claims reset when a player leaves/rejoins and daily at 00:00 UTC. The counter stops at the last tier (60 minutes). Time is counted on the server from the moment the save loads, in any zone. | 5 min 1,000 Gold · 10 min 25 Gems · 15 min 2,500 Gold · 20 min 40 Gems · 30 min 2 Promotion Stones · 45 min 5,000 Gold · 60 min 100 Gems |
| **Codes** | Typed into the Rewards window. Each code pays once per profile and may have an expiry time. Case, spaces and dashes are ignored. | Per code |

- **Playtime limits:** at most 7 tiers, the last at most 60 minutes (asserted in `RewardsConfig`).
- **Codes are server-only:** the list is `ServerScriptService/Core/RewardCodes`, never in `ReplicatedStorage`, so clients cannot read it. Attempts are limited to one every 2 seconds, and an unknown code gets the same answer as a malformed one. Retire a code by setting `Expires` in the past rather than deleting it.
- **Inviting friends** (plan B5): the Friend Boost strip in Rewards has an **INVITE** button, and while a boost is active a green **+N%** tag rides on the HUD's Gold chip; tapping it opens the invite prompt too (`FriendInvite`: Roblox's `PromptGameInvite`, shown only when `CanSendGameInviteAsync` allows). Sending an invite earns nothing by itself: the boost rewards playing together.
- **Friend Boost:** each Roblox friend in the same server gives **+10% Gold, up to +30%** (3 friends). It multiplies the same Gold the Market does (tower kills, floor bosses and banked relics) and stacks with the Market, affixes, events and climb streaks. Gems, Promotion Stones and drop chances are unaffected. Friendships are checked on the server when a player joins (`Player:IsFriendsWithAsync`); the count is on the `FriendBoost` player attribute and in the Rewards window, and a toast announces changes. In Studio, the Workspace attribute `DebugFriendBoost` (a friend count) stands in for real friends.
- **Remotes:** `Remotes.RewardsRemotes.RewardsRequest` (`GET`, `CLAIM_DAILY`, `CLAIM_WEEKLY`, `CLAIM_PLAYTIME` + tier, `REDEEM_CODE` + text; 4 requests per second) returns `{ Ok, Message, State }`; `RewardsSync` pushes the same State on join, at the daily reset and when the boost changes.
- **Save data:** `profile.Rewards = { Daily = { Streak, LastClaimDay }, Weekly = { LastClaimWeek }, Playtime = { Day, Seconds, Claimed }, Codes }` (schema v8, §8.3).
- **Potions and Battle Pass XP:** a reward may also pay potions (`Reward.Potions`, §4.9), so codes and future calendar entries can hand them out. Claiming a daily, weekly or playtime reward earns Battle Pass XP; codes do not.

### 4.9 Battle Pass & Potions

Opened from the **Pass** button on the Right Rail, which shows a badge with the number of pass rewards ready to claim. Tunables live in `BattlePassConfig` and `PotionConfig`; the rules are pure (`BattlePassRules`, `PotionRules`) and the server (`BattlePassService`, `PotionService`) decides every claim, purchase and potion.

**Battle Pass**

- **Seasons:** 28 days each, back to back on the UTC clock from Monday 16 November 2026 00:00 UTC (`BattlePassConfig.Epoch`). Season names cycle through `SeasonNames`. A new season resets XP, claims and Premium.
- **Levels:** 30 levels, 1,000 XP each. XP stops at level 30.
- **Two rows, one reward per level each:**
  - **Free:** every player. Gold, Gems, Promotion Stones and one potion every few levels (880 Gems, 16 Promotion Stones and 9 potions across the season).
  - **Premium:** unlocked for the current season by a Robux Developer Product (`ShopConfig.Pass`, `pass_premium`, 399 Robux fallback label; the live price is read from the product). 3,520 Gems, 41 Promotion Stones and 26 potions across the season. Buying it late still pays every premium level already reached. The rewards are fixed and shown before purchase, so it is not a paid random item.
- **Claims:** each reward is claimed once, from its card or with **CLAIM ALL**. When a season ends, rewards that were unlocked but not claimed are delivered automatically (premium ones only if Premium was owned), then the track resets.
- **XP sources** (`BattlePassConfig.Xp`):

  | Action | XP |
  | --- | --- |
  | Regular / Elite / Alpha kill | 5 / 20 / 60 |
  | Floor boss / Biome Boss | 60 / 150 |
  | First clear of a floor | +100 |
  | Relic banked | 25 |
  | Raid (qualified) | 200 |
  | Daily / weekly / playtime reward claimed | 150 / 500 / 30 |

- **Quests** (`BattlePassConfig.DailyQuests` / `WeeklyQuests`): simple goals on the window's **Quests** tab that pay Battle Pass XP. Every player has the same list. Daily quests reset at 00:00 UTC and weekly quests on Monday 00:00 UTC. A finished quest is claimed from its row or with **CLAIM ALL**; a finished quest left unclaimed is paid automatically when its day or week ends. Quest XP is multiplied by the Pass XP Potion at the moment it is paid. All quests together pay 4,200 XP a week from dailies and 3,600 from weeklies.

  | Daily quest | XP | Weekly quest | XP |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | Clear 3 tower floors | 150 | Clear 25 tower floors | 600 |
  | Summon a hero | 100 | Summon 10 heroes | 600 |
  | Summon an artifact at the Forge | 100 | Summon 10 artifacts at the Forge | 600 |
  | Defeat 50 enemies | 150 | Defeat 500 enemies | 600 |
  | Bank 2 relics | 100 | Bank 15 relics | 600 |
  | | | Defeat 2 Biome Bosses | 600 |

  A floor counts as cleared when its boss falls. Progress is counted on the server, from the same hooks that pay XP (`BattlePassConfig.QuestMetrics`) plus the hero and artifact summon handlers (`BattlePassService.Progress`).
- **Window:** two tabs, **Rewards** and **Quests**, each showing its count of ready claims. The window does not scroll up and down: the reward cards and the quest rows are sized to the height available (daily and weekly quests side by side), and only the reward track scrolls sideways. A screen too short for the minimum sizes falls back to scrolling. The Quests tab is two framed boards, **Daily** (violet) and **Weekly** (azure), each with a title band showing how many quests are done and the time to its reset. A quest row has an icon medallion, the goal, a slim progress bar with its count, and the XP as a gold pill; a finished quest turns gold with a CLAIM button, and a claimed one dims and shows a tick. On Rewards the premium row is gold (gilt cards over a gold wash, bright gold when a reward is ready), and a fixed **Final Reward** column on the right previews the level 30 reward of each row; tapping it scrolls the track to the end. The Pass button's badge counts ready rewards plus finished quests.
- **Purchase flow:** the window opens Roblox's checkout; `ShopService.ProcessReceipt` grants it through the same idempotent receipt handling as every Store product (§8.3), and confirms it once the profile is saved. A second Premium receipt in one season (a retry that slipped past, or a gift) is paid as 10 levels of XP rather than nothing. While `ProductId` is 0 the button shows COMING SOON; in Studio it unlocks the row directly (`DEV_UNLOCK`, refused outside Studio) for play-testing.
- **Remote:** `Remotes.BattlePassRemotes.BattlePassRequest` (`CLAIM` + track + level, `CLAIM_ALL`, `QUEST_CLAIM` + quest id, `QUEST_CLAIM_ALL`; 4 requests per second) returns `{ Ok, Message }`. The state reaches the client in the profile sync.
- **Save data:** `profile.BattlePass = { Season, Xp, Premium, Free, Paid, Quests }` (schema v11, §8.3). `Quests = { Day, Week, Progress, Claimed }` holds the UTC day and week the progress belongs to; `BattlePassRules.EnsureState` adds or repairs it on every load, so it needs no schema step of its own. `Free` and `Paid` are bitmasks of claimed levels, so the state stays small: XP changes on every kill and profile sync resends a changed key whole.

**Potions**

Potions are consumables kept in the Backpack's **Potions** tab. Drinking one starts a timed boost or adds its time to a running one, up to 4 hours. **Boost time only counts down while the player is in the game.** There is one potion per effect, so a boost never has two strengths.

| Potion | Effect | Duration |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Gold Potion** | +50% tower Gold (kills, floor bosses, banked relics). Multiplies with the Market, the friend boost, affixes, events and climb streaks. | 30 min |
| **Gem Potion** | +50% Gems from kills and banked relics. First-clear bonuses and raid payouts are not affected. | 30 min |
| **Luck Potion** | +25% summon luck on Call of the Rift and A Star's Arrival. It adds to God's Blessing and goes on top of its +50% cap. Pity is unchanged, Open Forge Roll Luck uses its own uncapped Fortress Tree branch, and the Robux banners (Gilded Mirror, Gilded Anvil) have no luck. | 15 min |
| **Hero XP Potion** | +100% combat XP for squad heroes (the Pit's per-grant cap still applies). | 30 min |
| **Pass XP Potion** | +50% Battle Pass XP from every source. | 30 min |

- **Sources:** both Battle Pass rows, and any reward that lists `Potions` (promo codes, for example). A profile holds at most 999 of each.
- **Odds disclosure:** every odds display (`SummonLuck.ForProfile`: the summon screen, the odds modal, the Index and the Forge) includes a running Luck Potion, so the shown odds always match the roll.
- **HUD:** each running boost shows a chip with its flask and time left beside the currency bar; tapping it opens the Backpack. The server sets each boost's end time on a player attribute (`Boost<Effect>Until`, server clock), so the client counts down without messages.
- **Remote:** `Remotes.PotionRemotes.PotionRequest` (`USE` + potion id; 4 requests per second) returns `{ Ok, Message }`.
- **Save data:** `profile.Potions = { [potionId] = count }` and `profile.Boosts = { [effect] = seconds left }` (schema v11). Loading a save repairs both: unknown ids are dropped and numbers are clamped.

---

### 4.10 Store Gold Packs
The three Robux Gold packs (`ShopConfig.Gold`: Coin Purse 19 R$, Gold Strongbox 79 R$, Dragon's Hoard 199 R$) grant the **larger** of their listed amount (100K / 520K / 1.68M) and **10 / 52 / 168 minutes of the buyer's frontier income**, so a pack keeps its value as the Master climbs instead of becoming worth less than one relic. Frontier income (`Util/FrontierIncome`) is a fixed model of base tower Gold per minute on the Master's highest cleared floor: 30 Regular and 3 Elite kills plus one banked Regular relic (the floor's Geode/Urn mix), without Market, streak, friend or potion bonuses; Floor 50 is about 1.6M Gold per minute. The server works out the amount when the receipt is granted (`ShopConfig.GrantAmount`), and the Store card shows the same number. Gem packs do not scale.

## 5. BIOME BOSS ENCOUNTER DESIGN (MILESTONE FLOORS)

### 5.1 Architecture & Biome Milestone Cadence
In **Infinite Tower**, major boss encounters take place at the apex of each biome: every 5th floor of the Infinite Tower (Floors 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45, 50). Each Biome Boss commands a massive dedicated arena dais ($60 \times 2 \times 60\text{ studs}$ `BossDaisFixture`) and possesses dynamic health, high damage multipliers, and signature multi-phase telegraphed attacks.

#### The 10 Biome Bosses:

| Floor | Biome Name | Biome Boss Name | Rig Family | Maximum Health | Basic Attack Damage | Signature Attack Types |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **5** | **Verdant Ruins** | **Mossbound Guardian** | `STONE_COLOSSUS` | `98,719` | `379` | Frontal Cleave (`ARC`), Quake (`SELF_DISC`), Root Bloom (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **10** | **Fungal Hollow** | **Spore Matriarch** | `FUNGAL_COLOSSUS` | `1,172,275` | `1,535` | Spore Burst (`SELF_DISC`), Corrosive Cloud (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **15** | **Ashen Dunes** | **Sandstone Colossus** | `STONE_COLOSSUS` | `6,929,813` | `4,868` | Magma Cleave (`ARC`), Sand Quake (`SELF_DISC`), Obsidian Spikes (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **20** | **Frozen Citadel** | **Frostbound King** | `ROYAL_BIPED` | `49,627,906` | `19,714` | Glacial Cleave (`ARC`), Blizzard Shock (`SELF_DISC`), Ice Spikes (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **25** | **Infernal Foundry** | **Ember Titan** | `FORGE_COLOSSUS` | `256,773,969` | `42,562` | Anvil Hammer (`ARC`), Slag Quake (`SELF_DISC`), Molten Core (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **30** | **Thunder Highlands** | **Storm Roc** | `GIANT_AVIAN` | `1,705,791,750` | `172,375` | Gale Slash (`ARC`), Thunderclap (`SELF_DISC`), Lightning Strike (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **35** | **Corrupted Grove** | **Blight Sovereign** | `GROVE_COLOSSUS` | `9,109,778,438` | `372,144` | Briar Whip (`ARC`), Heartrot Bloom (`SELF_DISC`), Rot Tendrils (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **40** | **Sunken Sanctuary** | **Tidal Leviathan** | `SERPENT` | `20,861,059,578` | `546,802` | Tidal Sweep (`ARC`), Maelstrom (`SELF_DISC`), Geyser Strike (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **45** | **Astral Archives** | **Rift Warden** | `ARCANE_COLOSSUS` | `83,159,446,094` | `546,802` | Astral Sweep (`ARC`), Dimensional Rupture (`SELF_DISC`), Void Well (`TARGET_DISC`) |
| **50** | **Abyssal Throne** | **Crimson Overlord** | `ROYAL_COLOSSUS` | `303,887,290,571` | `546,802` | Hellfire Cleave (`ARC`), Abyssal Nova (`SELF_DISC`), Doom Pillars (`TARGET_DISC`) |

### 5.2 Full 50-Enemy Biome Roster & Procedural Rig Architecture
Each of the 10 biomes features 4 local-authored procedural enemies (2 Regulars, 1 Elite, 1 Biome Boss = **40 enemies total** in `MobCatalog.luau` and `FirstBiomeCombat.luau`), built with biome-specific material/glow palettes in `TowerEnemyRigs.luau` and animated with planted-foot leg counter-rotation (`plantedPose`) in `TowerEnemyClips.luau`.

**Enemy animation playback** (`EnemyAnimator.luau`, docs/mob-reanimation-plan.md): one client clip player drives every enemy rig in the tower, the hub patrol and the Facility preview, writing `Motor6D.Transform`. Idle and walk blend by measured speed with hysteresis (walk starts above 0.6 studs/s and stops 0.25 s after dropping below 0.3), and each mob's idle phase and rate (×0.9–1.1) are seeded from its NetId so a camp never breathes in unison. Attacks play on top, keyed to the server timings: anticipation to the clip's `Release` marker, the strike in the last 15% of the windup (at most 0.25 s) reaching `Impact` exactly at the windup's end, the impact pose held through a projectile's flight, then recovery. Legs keep walking under an attack while the mob moves. Hit flinches are additive, so they play mid-chase and mid-windup; death overrides everything. Joints ease towards their targets (rate 14/s) except through a strike. Clip translations scale with the drawn hip height against a reference per clip group (biped 3.5, multi-leg 1.3, quadruped 2.4, avian 2.8, serpent 4.2 studs; clamped 0.4–4×), and a walk cycle covers `4 × hip × sin(swing)` studs (±24° bipeds and avians, ±22° quadrupeds), so feet stay planted at every size, Alphas included. Appendage joints named `Extra*` (tails, jaws, capes, censers...; registry in `tools/blender/mob_extras.py`) and Alpha `Trophy_*` joints carry a spring role in the model index (`Cloth`, `Hanging`, `Rigid`, `Spin`). While no playing clip keys them they lag behind their pivot's acceleration on a damped spring, or turn steadily; springs rest beyond 140 studs. Biped families use four motion sets (`EnemyClips/Bipeds.luau`): LightBiped (small, caster, fungal), WarriorBiped (weapon, armoured, royal), HeavyBiped and Rooted (rooted, treant), each with its own stance, idle, walk, death and fidgets (one every 6–14 s, seeded, after 3 s of calm). A move's style follows its shape (ARC slash/backhand/whip, TARGET_DISC overhead/pound/root burst, SELF_DISC stomp/uproot, PROJECTILE bolt/throw, support kits channel) with per-enemy overrides (Rime Sentry thrust, Forge Construct piston, Executioner chop, Taskmaster chain lash, Bellows Imp pumping), and clips resolve by `Slot:Shape`. Colossus bosses (and the raid bosses' stand-in rigs) use the Colossus set (`EnemyClips/Colossi.luau`): Sweep, Pound, Quake and Hurl by shape, a slumped-to-roaring awakening, and a kneel-then-crash death; the Spore Matriarch's crown rises for its signatures and the Rift Warden's tome shields swing in on each windup. Creatures use four sets (`EnemyClips/Creatures.luau`): Quadruped (diagonal trot; lunge-bite, pounce, howl-stomp; rams butt), Avian (folded wings, hop-walk; rake, dive, wing buffet, wing flick, the herald's cry), Serpent (S-wave coils; coil bite, rear strike, coil slam) and Multileg (tripod walk; snap, leap, drum; scorpions pinch and tail-stab). The Storm Roc and the Tidal Leviathan each have their own awakening, and tails, jaws, beaks, frills, stings and elytra move in every clip. Through an attack's windup the head turns towards the aim (up to 35°; quadrupeds and serpents turn their body too), walking mobs lean into turns (up to 7°), and beyond 140 studs fidgets, aim and lean are skipped like springs.


| Biome (Floors) | Regular 1 | Regular 2 | Biome Elite | Biome Boss (5th Floor) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1. Verdant Ruins (1–5)** | Moss Crawler (`CRAWLER`) | Ruin Guardian (`ARMORED_BIPED`) | Vinebound Elite (`ROOTED_BIPED`) | **Mossbound Guardian** (`STONE_COLOSSUS`) |
| **2. Fungal Hollow (6–10)** | Spore Skitter (`CRAWLER`) | Lantern Spore (`FUNGAL_BIPED`) | Mycelial Brute (`HEAVY_BIPED`) | **Spore Matriarch** (`FUNGAL_COLOSSUS`) |
| **3. Ashen Dunes (11–15)** | Dune Scarab (`INSECT`) | Ash Raider (`WEAPON_BIPED`) | Glass Scorpion (`SCORPION`) | **Sandstone Colossus** (`STONE_COLOSSUS`) |
| **4. Frozen Citadel (16–20)** | Frost Hound (`QUADRUPED`) | Rime Sentry (`WEAPON_BIPED`) | Glacier Knight (`ARMORED_BIPED`) | **Frostbound King** (`ROYAL_BIPED`) |
| **5. Infernal Foundry (21–25)** | Cinder Imp (`SMALL_BIPED`) | Forge Construct (`ARMORED_BIPED`) | Molten Taskmaster (`HEAVY_BIPED`) | **Ember Titan** (`FORGE_COLOSSUS`) |
| **6. Thunder Highlands (26–30)** | Storm Talon (`AVIAN`) | Crag Channeler (`CASTER_BIPED`) | Thunder Ram (`QUADRUPED`) | **Storm Roc** (`GIANT_AVIAN`) |
| **7. Corrupted Grove (31–35)** | Blight Wolf (`QUADRUPED`) | Thorn Weaver (`ROOTED_BIPED`) | Rotwood Keeper (`TREANT`) | **Blight Sovereign** (`GROVE_COLOSSUS`) |
| **8. Sunken Sanctuary (36–40)** | Tide Claw (`CRUSTACEAN`) | Drowned Acolyte (`CASTER_BIPED`) | Coral Warden (`ARMORED_BIPED`) | **Tidal Leviathan** (`SERPENT`) |
| **9. Astral Archives (41–45)** | Rune Mite (`CRAWLER`) | Astral Scribe (`CASTER_BIPED`) | Archive Executor (`ARMORED_BIPED`) | **Rift Warden** (`ARCANE_COLOSSUS`) |
| **10. Abyssal Throne (46–50)** | Abyss Reaver (`WEAPON_BIPED`) | Void Invoker (`CASTER_BIPED`) | Throne Executioner (`HEAVY_BIPED`) | **Crimson Overlord** (`ROYAL_COLOSSUS`) |

**Floor Alphas (10 more, Phase 6).** Each biome also has one **Alpha**, the boss of its floors 1–4: *Vinebound Alpha*, *Mycelial Brute Alpha*, *Glass Scorpion Alpha*, *Glacier Knight Alpha*, *Molten Taskmaster Alpha*, *Thunder Ram Alpha*, *Rotwood Keeper Alpha*, *Coral Warden Alpha*, *Archive Executor Alpha* and *Throne Executioner Alpha*. An Alpha reuses its biome Elite's rig family (1.4× larger, gilded), has its own stat tier between Elite and Boss (about 15× the squad DPS the curve is built on, against 8× and 25×), and adds a self-centred quake (`SELF_DISC`, 16 studs) to the Elite's cleave and targeted disc. It is summoned at the Apex when a Master's kill threshold is met; camp Elites never are. The roster is therefore 60 enemy templates (30 Regulars including the 10 supports, 20 Elite-role including the 10 Alphas, 10 Biome Bosses).

**Support Regulars (10 more, Phase D).** Each biome has one support Regular that casts for its camp instead of fighting: *Bloom Tender* (Verdant Ruins), *Sporecap Chanter* (Fungal Hollow), *Ember Oracle* (Ashen Dunes), *Rime Chaplain* (Frozen Citadel), *Bellows Imp* (Infernal Foundry), *Squall Herald* (Thunder Highlands), *Rot Mender* (Corrupted Grove), *Tide Cantor* (Sunken Sanctuary), *Glyph Warden* (Astral Archives) and *Void Hierophant* (Abyssal Throne). Each uses one kit (`SupportRules.luau`):

| Kit | Biomes | Effect | Numbers |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MEND` | Verdant, Corrupted, Sunken, Abyssal | Heals the ally with the lowest HP% (only allies below 95%) | 2.0 s windup, 12% of the ally's MaxHP, 7 s cooldown, range 30 |
| `WARD` | Ashen, Frozen, Astral | Shields the unshielded ally nearest the squad | 1.4 s windup, shield of 15% MaxHP for 6 s, 9 s cooldown, range 26 |
| `ROUSE` | Fungal, Infernal, Thunder | Allies within 18 studs get +25% ATK and +20% move speed | 1.2 s windup, 5 s, 10 s cooldown; cast once an ally in range is fighting |

Supports have 70% of a Regular's HP and 50% of its ATK, move at 10 and aggro at 46 studs. They keep 21 studs behind their nearest ally, on the side away from the squad, and only fire a weak bolt once no ally is left within 40 studs. **Any hero hit during a cast's windup interrupts it** and the cooldown stays spent, so Strikers and Marksmen counter them. About half the camps field one support (never more than half, re-rolled with each wave; `CampRoster.AssignSupports`), starting on floor 3; surge packs swap a Regular for a support from biome 3. Supports count toward the 48 live-mob budget, count as Regulars for kill points and relics, and wear a floating halo and a "Support" name tag. Shielded and roused mobs show a blue or amber bubble.


### 5.3 Tactical Mechanics & Combat Flow ("Greed vs. Coma")
1. **Telegraph Shapes & Timings (`AttackTimeline.luau`):**
   - **`ARC` (Basic Cleave):** Fast frontal swipe ($0.65\text{s–}1.1\text{s}$ windup, $7\text{–}13\text{-stud}$ reach). Vanguard tanks should position facing away from squishier DPS heroes.
   - **`SELF_DISC` (Boss Quake / Burst):** Expanding crimson circle with inner progress-fill disc centered on the boss ($1.8\text{s}$ windup, $1.2\text{s}$ recovery, 22-stud radius). Masters must sprint out and trigger **`[E]` Emergency Recall** before impact.
   - **`TARGET_DISC` (Targeted AoE):** Ground circle with expanding fill disc locked onto a hero's position ($1.4\text{s–}2.2\text{s}$ windup, $9\text{–}13\text{-stud}$ radius). Requires instant Master recognition and recall repositioning.
   - **`PROJECTILE` (Ranged Swept Corridor):** Aimed linear corridor ($0.75\text{s}$ windup + $0.35\text{s}$ travel time, $26\text{-stud}$ range, $2.2\text{-stud}$ corridor width). Evaluates authoritative swept-segment distance along the projectile line at `WindupSeconds + TravelSeconds`.
2. **Coma Skill Check:**
   - Every hero inside a shape when it lands takes the move's damage (the attacker's ATK × the move's multiplier, with support, affix and aura modifiers). A hero brought to 0 HP is **Downed** (sim state `COMA`) until the squad changes floor, goes home or wipes. The Skill Gauge is not touched.
3. **Punish Window:**
   - After a Signature lands, a tower boss stays in its recovery (1.2–1.5 s), unable to act. Active Masters trigger **`[Q]` Overdrive Whistle** here to maximise DPS. Tower bosses take no bonus damage in this window; the $+25\%$ damage-taken Punish Window (4 s) belongs to Raid Bosses only (§5.6).

### 5.4 Biome Boss Loot & Progression Ledger
- **Summoning, not respawning:** a floor's boss (the biome **Alpha** on floors 1–4, the **Biome Boss** on floor 5) is summoned for a Master whose own kill progress reaches the floor threshold. It never respawns on a timer. Repeat kills need a fresh threshold.
- **Kill Gems** (every kill, paid straight to the killer, `TowerFloorConfig.KillGemsByRole`): Regular 1, Elite 5, Alpha 10, Biome Boss 50, each × `1 + 0.02 × (floor − 1)` (`KillGemFloorScaling`, rounded). Floor 50 pays 2 / 10 / 20 / 99. Relic Gems scale faster (`RelicGemFloorScaling` 0.03, ×2.47 at floor 50), so relics gain value relative to kills as you climb. Kill Gold keeps `1.055^(floor − 1)`.
- **First clear of a floor** (the big payout): Gold ×3, `10 + 2 × floor` bonus Gems on top of the kill Gems, and a guaranteed Relic tagged to the killer.
- **Repeat kills:** Gold at the normal rate, a chance of a relic based on floor and enemy role (§5.5), and only the kill Gems (no first-clear bonus).
- **Boss relics:** Alphas and Biome Bosses are the only source of the **Calamity Heart** (Alpha 2% → 5% of their relics, Biome Boss 4% → 10%); the rest are Cursed Urns and Mana Geodes. A first Biome Boss clear always drops at least a Cursed Urn.
- **First clear of a Biome Boss** (floors 5, 10, …, 50), on top of the above: `300 × biome number` Gems. Bosses, Alphas and other mobs never drop artifacts: every non-Robux artifact is rolled and purchased on the Open Forge pedestals (§7.4).
- **Floor advancement:** defeating a floor's boss opens the next floor on the Floor Selector and raises the Master's Rift Attunement cap (Section 2.5).

### 5.5 Relic Drops
Relics are the contested tower loot. Drop odds depend on the enemy role and interpolate between Floors 1, 11 and 50. A qualifying first clear guarantees the killer a relic. See the [tower relic loot table](balance/RELIC_LOOT_TABLE.md) for odds, base payouts by biome and tier, and examples.

- **Floor scaling:** each floor above Floor 1 adds 1% of base Gems and 5% of base Gold. The Market multiplies Gold, and the climb streak multiplies both. The relic's billboard shows its base value at that floor.
- **Billboard themes** (`Config/BillboardThemes`): world billboards have **no plate or badge**, so they never cover the fight. The colour is the quality. A relic shows three short lines: its name in the rarity colour (Mana Geode azure, Cursed Urn amber, Abyssal Core void purple, Calamity Heart blood red; gradient titles from the Urn up), its value at this floor (`120 Gems · 400 Gold`), and a status line that stays empty unless the relic is sealed (`Sealed 4s`), locked, carried (the carrier's name) or within 20 s of despawning (`Vanishes in 12s`). A biome skin keeps its tier's colours. Higher tiers get larger text and longer view range, and Cores and Hearts draw on top of walls. Mob nameplates are the name over a slim HP bar, both in the tier colour: Normal red on slate, Elite void purple, Alpha gilded gold, Biome Boss blood red (larger as the tier rises; a support mob's name ends in `✚`). Hero nameplates are the name and level in the inherent rarity colour (Common slate, Uncommon jade, Rare azure, Superior violet, Exalted amber, Legendary gold, Mythical blood red; gradient titles from Exalted up) over an HP bar in the same colour and a thin gold skill gauge, with the hero's star rank under the bars (earned stars in gold, the rest of the 7 dim). Duel colours replace both during a retaliation duel. **Top-centre strip** (`FloorBossTopBanner`, no panel): on a floor it reads `FLOOR 3 · NAME` over a thin kill-progress line and `3 / 14`, and adds `REC. POWER` in red only when the squad is under the recommendation; against a boss it becomes the boss name in the boss tier colours over a wide ink-outlined HP bar with the percentage inside and a pale damage trail, with `ENRAGED` and the raid timer under it.
- **A new Master's first relic:** the first clear of Floor 1 pays a relic to every Master who qualifies for the boss's rewards, not only the one who landed the kill. That relic is **protected**: nobody but its owner can carry it, for as long as it exists (its status line reads `Protected`), so it can't be stolen and opens no bounty. Every later relic can be stolen as normal (`RelicService` `ProtectedForUserId`).
- **Seal and owner lock:** a fresh relic drop is **sealed for everyone, its killer included**, for `5s` (Calamity Heart `15s`): its Carry prompt stays off and the billboard counts the seal down, then anyone may take it. A fumbled relic is locked to the player who fumbled it for `5s`.
- **Calamity Heart bonus:** extracting any biome's Heart has a 10% chance to grant a free **Sovereign Archon** (Exalted, summoned at 1★ like every hero).
- **Biome skins:** `TowerFloorConfig.RollRelic` rolls a tier, then returns the floor's biome skin of it (`RelicConfig.Biomes[i].DropTable`, ids like `VERDANT_GEODE` … `THRONE_HEART`, 40 in `RelicConfig.Items`). Each biome has its own appearance and payouts. Speed penalty, jump lock, seal, despawn, billboard plate and model shape follow the base tier. Names run Geode / Urn / Core / Heart in every biome, e.g. Verdant Ruins: *Overgrown Moss Geode*, *Cursed Verdant Urn*, *Rootbound Earth Core*, *Heart of the Mossbound*; Abyssal Throne: *Abyssal Trench Geode*, *Sovereign's Cursed Urn*, *Void Titan Core*, *Heart of the Crimson Overlord*.
- **Income target:** about 2,900 / 6,800 / 12,700 Gems an hour of repeat farming on Alpha floors 1 / 26 / 49, with Biome Boss floors paying a little more (`tests/tower-run-loop.spec.luau`).
- **Visuals:** the server replicates an invisible relic root; each client builds the relic's model (`RelicModelBuilder`: a cracked crystal geode, a rune-banded urn, a caged void core, a crystal-spiked heart) with an outline glow, point light, rising motes, a vertical loot beam, a pulsing ground ring, idle bob and spin, and a spawn burst (`RelicVisualController`). Rarer tiers get a brighter light, taller beam and more motes; the beam, ring and bob hide while the relic is carried.

### 5.6 Raid Bosses
Implemented by `RaidConfig`, `RaidRules`, `RaidService`, `RaidArenaBuilder`, `RaidPortalKit` and `Hud/RaidHud`. Full plan: `docs/raid-boss-plan.md`.

- **Schedule:** a raid starts at every :00 and :30 UTC, on every server. 60 s before, the **Raid Portal** in the Commons opens and the whole server is told. The fight lasts **5 minutes**, then a 12 s results screen, then everyone still in the arena returns to the portal. Entry closes 30 s before the end.
- **Bosses** rotate in a fixed order: **Vorthak, the Hollow King**, **Ozrith, the Thousand-Eyed**, **Xal'Zhara, the Rift Broodmother**, **Aurelith, the Shardbound Colossus**. Each has a basic attack, two signatures and an enrage move that unlocks in Phase 2 (at 50% HP or 3:00 elapsed). Signatures open a 4 s **Punish Window** (+25% damage taken). The Broodmother summons six spiderlings when she enrages; Aurelith takes +50% damage while enraged.
- **Entry:** the Tower must be unlocked, the Master needs a standing hero, and can't be carrying a relic, flagged as a thief, duelling or travelling. Any tower assignment is dropped and the whole squad travels with the Master.
- **Arena:** off-map at `(-14000, 1000, -14000)`, floor id 60. A circular 320-stud floor with the boss dais to the north and a `CombatSafe` staging pad to the south, behind a gate that opens when the boss wakes. The arena takes the boss's colours.
- **Boss HP** is built from the people who show up: each participant adds `squad DPS × 300 s × 0.75` (minimum DPS 25). Late joiners add their share while keeping the HP fraction, so a dying boss never heals. Boss ATK is half a Biome Boss's hit at the participants' median best floor.
- **Rewards** are paid once per raid, by damage share: `Gems = min(2000, round(2000 × kill × (0.10 + 0.90 × share^0.6)))`, where `share` is your damage over the top damager's and `kill = 0.5 + 0.5 × fraction of HP removed` (1 on a kill). Only a kill pays the full **2,000 Gems** to the top damager; every qualifier (top 10, or at least 0.5% of max HP) gets at least 10%. Gold is `Gems × 25 × (1 + 0.05 × (best floor − 1))`. There are no relic or other Gem extras, so 2,000 Gems is a hard cap. `profile.Raid.LastRewardedRaid` makes the payout idempotent.
- **No contested loot:** raid mobs pay no kill rewards, drop no relics and give no floor progress. A squad wipe sends the Master home with their damage still counted, with no wipe loss.

---

## 6. CONTESTED LOOT & BOUNTY PVP

### 6.1 Contested Loot & Bounty Duels
Implemented by `LootDuel` (`Shared/Util`, rules and arena layout), `DuelArena` (`Combat`, arena geometry), the duel section of `CombatSimulation` and `DuelController.client.luau`. Original spec: `docs/rework-plan/PHASE_3_TOWER_PVP_RETALIATION.md` (the owner-only retaliation it describes was replaced by the bounty hunt below).

1. **Relics are the only physical drops:** Kills pay their Gold and Gems directly; the old physical Gold/Gem cargo (`Workspace.PhysicalLoot`, Bounty Bag) is removed. The contested drops are the killer-tagged **Relics** (`Workspace.PhysicalRelics`), which must be carried to a sanctuary to bank. `RelicService` keeps the killer on each relic (`KillerUserId`) and reports every relic pickup to `CombatSimulation.ReportRelicPickup`, which opens the bounty on a theft. Contests are keyed by relic id.
2. **Physical Overhead Hauling:** Picking up loot welds it overhead, slows movement by `-15%` to `-45%`, and triggers the upper-arm holding pose (`HoldingAnimationController.client.luau`).
3. **Theft Detection:** Picking up a drop tagged to someone else opens a **contest** for that drop and flags the picker as a `[Loot Thief]` (character attribute `IsLootThief` plus a nameplate everyone sees) for 3 minutes. The flag is an open **bounty**: the thief carries a `Claim Bounty [F]` ProximityPrompt (`BountyPrompt`, 12-stud range, hidden on the thief's own client) and every other Master on the floor gets a bounty toast. The owner also gets a banner with a countdown and an `HUNT THEM: [F]` hint (`TowerRemotes.LootContested`). There is no Gold reward: the prize is the drop itself. The contest closes when the window runs out, the drop is banked or despawns, or the owner picks it back up. If the drop is stolen again, only the newest thief carries the bounty.
4. **Bounty Duel:** Any Master except the thief (the robbed owner included) walks up to the thief and presses **F**. The duel starts at once; the server re-checks range, so the hunter must really be next to the thief. A thief with several bounties is challenged for the newest steal. Both Masters must be on the same floor with at least one hero standing. Clicking another Master never starts a fight. There is no open-field PvP: squads only fight each other inside a duel.
   - **Arena:** A floating hex-grid island is built just under the room's ceiling bound, above the hunter, so it stays inside room-local replication and clear of floor geometry. Each squad lines up in formation on its own half: melee (Vanguard, Berserker, Striker) in a front row of three, ranged and support (Marksman, Scholar, Healer) in a back row of two. Each client gets a fixed high-angle camera from behind its own squad, with its heroes labelled green and the opponent orange (`DuelState` remote).
   - **Flow:** 2.5 s line-up, then up to 40 s of automatic combat with each hero's own kit at 50% hero-vs-hero damage. Mobs ignore duelling squads. Masters are held in place, cannot bank or recall, and the drop cannot be picked up by anyone.
   - **Victory:** Knock out every opposing hero. On timeout, the side with more total HP fraction left wins (a tie goes to the thief, who defends). A Master who leaves, travels or changes floor forfeits.
3a. **Rival Masters** (the onboarding lesson, §4.7): a duel side can be a server-run rival instead of a player. A rival is a stand-in Master (negative user id, no character, profile or tower state) whose heroes are ordinary sim heroes created for its duel and removed after it (`CombatSimulation.CreateRival`, `ArmRival`, `StartRivalDuel`, `DismissRival`). It never forfeits unless dismissed, its floor stands in for a profile's in the Attunement cap, and its relic's theft opens a training contest (above). `CombatSimulation.DebugRivalDuel(player, roster?)` starts one in Studio.
4a. **Carry Challenge:** Any Master can also challenge a Master who is **carrying** a relic, without a theft first. Every carrier has a `Steal Relic [F]` prompt (`StealPrompt`, short hold, 12-stud range, hidden on their own client); pressing it starts a **Relic Duel** at once with the same arena and rules as a bounty duel: the challenger hunts (side A), the carrier defends (side B), the winner takes the relic and the loser is locked out of it for its lifetime. The carrier is never flagged a thief and no bounty opens (`LootDuel.NewCarryContest`, `Kind = "CARRY"`). A thief's stolen relic is still claimed through its bounty prompt instead. Guardrails, all checked on the server (`LootDuel.CanChallengeCarrier`, `CombatSimulation.challengeCarrier`):
   - **Empty hands:** the challenger can't be carrying a relic. A challenge risks a lockout and a cooldown, and nobody holds two relics.
   - **Same Tower floor only** (1–50), and never in or against anyone standing in a sanctuary or combat-safe zone, so nobody is pinned at the door they are banking through.
   - **Protected relics** (a new Master's first, §5.5) can't be challenged. A relic already in a duel can't be challenged again.
   - **No interrupted boss fights:** a carrier who has hurt the floor's living boss can't be challenged.
   - **No farming low floors:** a Master can't carry-challenge on a floor 10 or more below their best clear (`CARRY_OVERLEVEL_FLOORS`). This also stops a strong account taking relics from a low-floor alt account.
   - **Full HP:** both squads fight a Relic Duel at full HP (restored afterwards), so nobody can ambush a carrier a boss or a camp has just worn down. Rift Attunement still holds both squads to the lower Master's cap.
   - **Shield:** whoever holds the relic after any duel can't be carry-challenged for 45 s (`CARRY_SHIELD_SECONDS`), so one challenger after another can't pin them down.
   - **Cooldowns:** a challenger waits 120 s after a loss and 30 s after a win before challenging anyone again. The same two Masters can't carry-duel each other again for 10 minutes (`CARRY_PAIR_COOLDOWN`), which stops harassing one player and passing relics between friends or alts.
   - **The relic's clock stops:** a relic's despawn timer is pushed back by the length of every duel fought over it (bounty duels too), so duels can't run it out.
   - **Spam:** the 3 s challenge cooldown and the server's own range check apply as for bounty claims. Refusals are toasts naming the guardrail (`"Shielded|32s after their last duel"`).
   - *Considered, not built:* evening out squad sizes. A Master with more squad slots fields more heroes; benching the extra heroes for the duel has no safe state for them, and Attunement plus the floor rule cover the worst gaps.
4b. **Duels as a show** (plan B2): through the 2.5 s line-up a **VS card** shows both Masters' headshots, names and squad power as they will fight (after the Attunement clamp; `PowerA`/`PowerB` in `DuelState` START). The duel is titled **BOUNTY DUEL** or **RELIC DUEL**, and the result names the prize ("You escaped with the Calamity Heart.", "You kept your Mana Geode."; a Rival lesson: "The Rival lets you keep the Mana Geode."). Through the outro the winners' heroes **pose**: the hero sim state `VICTORY` (wire state 5) plays each weapon's looping `Victory` clip (`HeroClips`: bow raised, sword up, daggers crossed overhead, axe hoisted, staff raised, tome hand thrown up), and `finishDuel` puts them back. **Other Masters on the floor** get a **WATCH DUEL** button (`DuelState` NEARBY / NEARBY_END) that frames the arena from side A until they stop or the duel ends; players in the Hub only get the result headline (server news), since room-local replication only sends a floor's entities to Masters on it. Duel results between two Masters are server news (§4.6.1).
5. **Zero-Consequence Resolution:** Duel knockouts never cause a squad wipe, teleport or progression loss. Afterwards every hero returns to its Master at its pre-duel HP and Skill Gauge.
   - **Winner:** Becomes the drop's owner. The drop is set at the winner's feet and picked up for them if their hands are free. The thief's flag clears.
   - **Loser lockout (hunter or thief alike):** The loser is locked out of that drop for the rest of its lifetime, whichever role they had. A losing hunter cannot claim it, and a losing thief cannot steal it again. A robbed owner who did not fight is not locked out. The lockout travels with the drop when it is dropped or spilled.
6. **Squad Wipe & Spilled Loot:** On a PvE squad defeat, a carried relic is fumbled onto the ground, and field looting is locked until the Master returns to their Fortress Plot (there is no Infirmary; see §4.3). The run ends with a wipe: the streak is lost and 25% of the Gold and Gems earned since the last bank is lost.
7. **Hub Banking:** Reaching a hub banking zone (the Cloister Commons, a plot bridge or the Pilgrim's Causeway), a Floor Safe Sanctuary or the private plot pad safely banks loot into permanent Gold and Gems.
8. **Notoriety** (plan B6; `NotorietyRules`, `NotorietyService`, `profile.Notoriety`, schema v10): each Master counts **thefts** (a relic picked up from someone else), **bounties claimed** (a hunter wins a bounty duel) and **escapes** (the thief wins one). Only fair fights count, or the boards would reward griefing:
   - never a Rival lesson's training theft or duel, never a Relic Duel (carry challenge), and never a server-run rival;
   - never between Roblox friends (`IsFriendsWith`, checked when the act is recorded);
   - never on a floor 10 or more below the actor's best clear (`OVERLEVEL_FLOORS`, as the carry-challenge rule), so a veteran robbing newcomers or a main farming alt accounts on Floor 2 earns nothing;
   - at most **3 counted acts against the same Master per UTC day** (`DAILY_CAP`), across all three kinds;
   - protected first relics (§5.5) can't be stolen at all, so they never count.
   - **Titles:** the thief track (thefts plus escapes) gives **Pickpocket** at 5 and **Notorious Thief** at 25; the hunter track (bounties claimed) gives **Bounty Hunter** at 5 and **Warden** at 25. The further track wins (a tie in rungs goes to the larger count). The title is a line over the Master's head (a server `BillboardGui`, `NotorietyTag`, under the `[Loot Thief]` tag; red for thieves, gold for hunters) and the `NotorietyTitle` player attribute.
   - **Weekly boards:** **Top Thieves** (thefts and escapes) and **Top Hunters** (bounties claimed) for the ISO week (Monday to Sunday, UTC). Each is one `OrderedDataStore` per week (`NotorietyThefts_2026W47`, `NotorietyHunts_2026W47`; Studio uses the `STUDIO` scope). A Master's weekly totals are written every 60 s when they changed and when they leave, not on every act; one server loop reads the top 10 of each every 60 s and pushes them to every client (`NewsRemotes.Boards`). They show as tabs on the HUD's Wanted panel (§4.6.1). *Built in the HUD instead of the planned two Leaderboard Pavilion slots: thefts happen in the tower, where the pavilion can't be seen. A pavilion board can show the same data later.*

---

## 7. EQUIPPABLE ARTIFACT & BLACKSMITH SYSTEM

### 7.1 Inherent Rarity, Progressive Stars & Inventory Limits

An **artifact is a weapon**, with one equipped artifact UUID per hero. Its rarity is an immutable property assigned on acquisition. **Star Promotion is the only upgrade to an owned weapon.** There are no enhancement levels or enhancement-fodder multipliers in the stat model.

1. **Inherent Rarity:** Sets base weapon power and a fixed attack-speed bonus across 7 launch tiers (`Common` to `Mythical`, scalable up to 16 tiers). Rarity cannot be upgraded, rerolled, or refined on the same UUID.
2. **Progressive Stars (1★ to 7★):** Multiply power by **1.00 / 1.60 / 2.50 / 4.00 / 6.50 / 10.00 / 16.00x**. Each promotion retains the recipient's rarity, template, and owner.
3. **1★ Universal Baseline:** All summoned weapons drop at **1★** regardless of their inherent rarity tier. Star promotion from $1★ \to 7★$ is conducted exclusively at the Blacksmith Forge by sacrificing donor weapons of the same rarity tier and star rank (regardless of weapon type). Promotion needs 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 and 6 donors per step (21 to reach 7★).
4. **Hard 50-Item Capacity Limits:**
   - Heroes and Artifacts share a baseline inventory limit of **50 items each** (`StatBalance.MaxHeroInventory = 50`, `StatBalance.MaxArtifactInventory = 50`).
   - If a Master's current inventory plus the incoming summon count exceeds 50, the transaction is rejected by the server with an inventory full notice.
5. **Artifact Liquidation (Selling for Gold):**
   - Masters can sell unequipped, unlocked artifacts directly from the Blacksmith Forge Assign tab for Gold.
   - Payout follows: $\text{SellPrice}(\text{Rarity}, \text{Star}) = \text{BasePrice}(\text{Rarity}) \times (1 + 0.5 \times (\text{Star} - 1))$.
   - Equipped artifacts and locked/favorite artifacts cannot be sold.

$$\text{WeaponPower} = \text{InherentRarityPower} \times \text{StatMultiplier} \times \text{WeaponStarMultiplier}$$
$$\text{WeaponDamageMultiplier} = 1 + \frac{\text{WeaponPower}}{40}$$

At **Mythical 7★ (Standard Variant)**, power is $7,000 \times 1.00 \times 16 = 112,000$ (a **2,801x trained-damage multiplier**), with a base $+30.0\%$ attack speed bonus. The **Enhanced Variant** scales to $8,400 \times 16 = 134,400$ power (**3,361x multiplier**) with $+35.0\%$ attack speed.

### 7.2 The 84-Weapon Catalog Structure (2 per Archetype per Rarity Tier)

The artifact catalog features an exhaustive **84-Weapon Roster** (6 Hero Archetypes $\times$ 7 Rarity Tiers $\times$ 2 Variants), guaranteeing every hero class has viable weapon progression at every tier:
- **Standard Variant:** $1.00\times$ base power, standard attack speed bonus ($60\%$ chance within tier).
- **Enhanced Variant:** $1.20\times$ base power, $+2.0\%$ to $+5.0\%$ bonus attack speed ($40\%$ chance within tier).

| Tier | Rarity | Standard Base Power (1★) | Standard ATK Speed | Enhanced Base Power (1★) | Enhanced ATK Speed | 1★ Base Sell Price |
| :---: | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: |
| **1** | **Common** | 20 | $+5.0\%$ | 24 | $+6.0\%$ | 100 Gold |
| **2** | **Uncommon** | 60 | $+7.5\%$ | 72 | $+9.0\%$ | 250 Gold |
| **3** | **Rare** | 180 | $+10.0\%$ | 216 | $+12.0\%$ | 600 Gold |
| **4** | **Superior** | 540 | $+13.0\%$ | 648 | $+15.0\%$ | 1,500 Gold |
| **5** | **Exalted** | 1,400 | $+16.5\%$ | 1,680 | $+18.5\%$ | 5,000 Gold |
| **6** | **Legendary** | 3,500 | $+20.0\%$ | 4,200 | $+22.5\%$ | 12,000 Gold |
| **7** | **Mythical** | 7,000 | $+30.0\%$ | 8,400 | $+35.0\%$ | 40,000 Gold |

Sell prices are kept low on purpose: at the +50% luck cap, selling everything a pull gives returns under 15% of its cost (a spec enforces this), so summoning and selling can never make Gold.

*(Full weapon names, wielders, and concept art descriptions across all 84 items are indexed in `ArtifactCatalog.luau` and the Artifact Index compendium `HeroIndex.luau`)*.

Higher rarities (Tiers 8–16) retain stat formulas in [the complete weapon matrix](balance/STAT_SCALING_TABLES.md#weapon-power-by-inherent-rarity-and-promoted-stars) for future expansions; launch gameplay focuses on Tiers 1–7.

### 7.3 Weapon Star Promotion (1★ to 7★)

Promotion consumes other weapons of the **same rarity tier and star rank** (any weapon type, e.g., swords, daggers, bows, axes, staves, tomes), plus the listed Gold/Gems (`WeaponPromotion.IsDonorFor`). The Gold in the table is the base price and is multiplied by the weapon's rarity factor, the same table as hero promotion (×1 Common and Uncommon, ×1.5 Rare, ×3 Superior, ×8 Exalted, ×25 Legendary, ×60 Mythical and above, `StatBalance.WeaponPromotionCost`). Donors and Gems are not scaled. The recipient's inherent rarity never changes. Reject locked, equipped, duplicate, or recipient-as-donor UUIDs. Consumed donors return no previous investment. No random success roll applies.

| Result stars | Power multiplier | Donors for this step | Gold | Gems |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 1.00x | — | — | — |
| 2 | 1.60x | 1 | 500 | 0 |
| 3 | 2.50x | 2 | 2,000 | 0 |
| 4 | 4.00x | 3 | 8,000 | 0 |
| 5 | 6.50x | 4 | 25,000 | 50 |
| 6 | 10.00x | 5 | 75,000 | 150 |
| 7 | 16.00x | 6 | 250,000 | 500 |

The full 1★ to 7★ path consumes 21 donor weapons plus the retained original. There is no additional enhancement-fodder sink. The multipliers now supply the full weapon progression that the removed enhancement track previously shared. Prices and acquisition pacing remain proposed tuning.

### 7.4 Open Forge 3D Rolling System & Relic-Scaled Pedestal Pricing
- **Open-Air 3D Forge (`Building_Blacksmith`):**
  - **Forge Smelter:** `[F]` `ProximityPrompt` opens the Smelter Workbench (`ForgeWorkbenchController.luau`) for Artifact Inventory, Equipping, Locking, Selling, Star Ascension ($1★ \to 7★$), live **Odds & Prices**, and a shortcut to the Forge Fortress Tree branches.
  - **Master Anvil (Free Rerolls):** Interactive via **both** clicking (`ClickDetector`, 30-stud server distance check) and `[F]` `ProximityPrompt` (`0.22s` server rate limit). Rerolling costs **0 Gold** and refreshes **all** unlocked pedestals simultaneously (no individual pedestal lock, no 10-pull or 120-pull pity counters).
  - **3D Artifact Pedestals (1 Starter, up to 6 Max):** Unlocked pedestals (`Pedestal_1..6`, spaced $\ge 7.16\text{ studs}$ apart) display a spinning 3D weapon model (`ForgePedestalController.client.luau`) with rarity VFX, a world-space top label showing **Rarity** and **Artifact Name** (plus Gold cost & Power on the owner's plot), and an `[F]` `ProximityPrompt` (`BuyArtifactPrompt`) when walking near that pedestal to purchase it. Buying clears only that pedestal slot while keeping other slots intact.
  - **Forge Auto-Roll GamePass (`ShopConfig.Passes.ForgeAutoRoll`, `199 Robux`):** Permanent account GamePass verified server-side (`ShopService` + `ForgeRollService`). When toggled ON in the proximity `ForgeRollHud` bar, the server automatically rerolls unlocked pedestals every `0.85s` while the Master is on the Hub/Island until any pedestal matches the chosen target `MinRarity` (`Rare+` through `Mythical`) and optional `WeaponType`, then pauses with a pedestal highlight & toast so the Master walks over to buy it.
- **Fortress Tree Forge Branches (`FortressTreeConfig.luau`):**
  - **`ForgePedestals` Branch (Max 6):** Five sequential `LEVEL` nodes (`fp_1..fp_5`, `2,500` to `450,000 Gold`, Floor `0..30` gates) raising active pedestals from `1` starter to `6` max.
  - **`ForgeLuck` Branch (Uncapped Roll Luck):** Separate from `GodsBlessing` hero Summon Luck. Four foundation `PERK` nodes (`rl_1..rl_4`, totaling `+50%` Roll Luck) lead into **`rl_endless` ("Eternal Stoking")**, an infinitely `REPEATABLE` node (`50,000 × 1.22^Rank` Gold, `+5%` Roll Luck per rank with **no maximum cap**).
- **Base Per-Pedestal Rarity Rates & Uncapped Odds-Ratio Luck (`RollLuck.luau`):**
  - Base rates (`ArtifactCatalog.RollRates`): **Common** `54.9%`, **Uncommon** `25.0%`, **Rare** `12.0%`, **Superior** `5.5%`, **Exalted** `2.0%`, **Legendary** `0.5%`, **Mythical** `0.1%`.
  - Uncapped Roll Luck $L \in [0, \infty)$ scales cumulative upper-tail odds $C_t(0) = \sum_{k=t}^{7} p_k$:
    $$C_t(L) = \frac{C_t(0)(1 + L)}{1 + C_t(0)L}, \qquad p'_t(L) = C_t(L) - C_{t+1}(L)$$
    Every tier probability stays strictly in $(0, 1)$ and sums to `1.0` at any finite luck rank.
- **Relic-Floor Scaled Gold Pedestal Prices (`ArtifactCatalog.GetPedestalCost`):**
  - Pedestal purchase costs use **Gold only**, anchored to 1 banked **biome Cursed Urn Relic** payout (`RelicConfig.Items[biome.DropTable[2]].Payout.Gold × TowerFloorConfig.RelicFloorMultipliers(TargetFloor)`) across milestone floors `{ 1, 8, 15, 20, 28, 40, 50 }`, with a `1.30×` multiplier for Enhanced (`1.20×` stat) variants:
    - **Common (F1):** `625` Standard / `813` Enhanced Gold
    - **Uncommon (F8):** `2,630` / `3,420` Gold
    - **Rare (F15):** `7,650` / `9,950` Gold
    - **Superior (F20):** `22,400` / `29,100` Gold
    - **Exalted (F28):** `91,700` / `119,000` Gold
    - **Legendary (F40):** `451,000` / `586,000` Gold
    - **Mythical (F50):** `1,750,000` / `2,280,000` Gold
  - 1★ sell prices (`100` to `40,000 Gold`) remain strictly `<= 16%` of pedestal purchase cost across all tiers, preventing free-reroll buy-and-sell Gold farming.
- **Two-Stage Roll Hierarchy:**
  1. Roll Rarity Tier (1–7) using `RollLuck.Roll(ArtifactCatalog.RollRates, rollLuck)`.
  2. Roll Archetype uniformly (1 of 6: Vanguard, Striker, Marksman, Berserker, Scholar, Healer; starter guarantee on a player's first unrolled weapon matches a deployed squad hero).
  3. Roll Variant (60% Standard, 40% High-Stat Enhanced).
  4. Persist rolled template ID on the pedestal; instantiate weapon at **1★** upon purchase.
- **Artifact Index Compendium:** All 84 weapons are cataloged in `HeroIndex.luau` under the Index's **ARTIFACTS** tab, with live collection tracking (`Collected X / 84 artifacts`), category filtering, 3D previews, and each weapon's Open Forge acquisition line (pedestal Gold price, Relic floor anchor, exact per-pedestal chance with Roll Luck, and 1★ sell price).
- **The Backpack is the inventory:** Roblox's own backpack (tool hotbar and the `` ` `` inventory) is disabled (`CoreGuiType.Backpack`; the game has no Tools), and `` ` `` or **I** opens and closes the Backpack instead, as does the Left Rail button. It is a slot grid with four tabs (Artifacts, Heroes, Materials, Potions): each slot shows the item's art on a rarity-coloured tile, its name and a badge (stars, level or stack count), with a cyan mark on items in use (an equipped artifact, a squad hero, a running boost). Empty slots pad the grid to whole rows. **Sort** cycles Rarity, Stars and Name. Selecting a slot shows its details beside the grid (on narrow screens, in place of it), with **DRINK** for potions.
- **Inventory caps in the Backpack:** the Backpack tabs read `Artifacts X/cap` and `Heroes X/cap` (50 plus the Storage branch, facility workers not counted), turning amber 5 below the cap and red at it, with a note on where to make room.

### 7.4a Premium Artifact Banner: "The Gilded Anvil" (Robux)
A Robux-only banner (`ArtifactCatalog.AnvilBanner` / `AnvilBanner`) featuring six **Gildforged** weapons, one per archetype: Dawnwarden's Oath (`SWORD_SHIELD`), Eclipse Sisters (`DAGGERS`), Aurora Skyhook (`BOW`), Wyrmheart Cleaver (`AXE`), Orrery of the Ninth Sky (`STAFF`) and Evergarden Codex (`TOME`). All six sit at Transcendent (tier 8, 10,000 Power at 1★ to 160,000 at 7★), support a customizable Focus archetype pick (`40%` Focus / `12%` each other, 10-pull Focus guarantee, and 60-pull Spark pick), cannot be sold for Gold, and ship as sculpted `EditableMesh` assets (`GildedAnvilMeshData` / `WeaponModelBuilder`) with **Band 6** VFX (`WeaponVfxConfig`, `WeaponVfx`, `GildedAnvilFx`: moving centrepieces, footer sigils, equip flourishes, dual gold trails and signature skill finishers), wired through `ShopConfig.Anvil`, `ShopService`, `DataManager.RollAnvilSummons`, `ShopPanel` and `SummonReveal`. Full specs and resolved design decisions: `docs/gilded-anvil-plan.md`.

### 7.5 Archetype Restrictions & 3D Socketing Standards
1. **One Weapon Type per Archetype:** Each hero holds a single equipped artifact, and each archetype can equip exactly one weapon type. There are no off-hand or accessory slots.

   | Archetype | Weapon type | Play pattern |
   | --- | --- | --- |
   | `Vanguard` | **Sword & Shield (`SWORD_SHIELD`)** | Main-hand slashing with an off-hand shield for defensive posture and damage mitigation. |
   | `Striker` | **Daggers (`DAGGERS`)** | High-speed dual-wield flurry attacks. |
   | `Marksman` | **Bow (`BOW`)** | Ranged single-target piercing attacks. |
   | `Berserker` | **Greataxe (`AXE`)** | Two-handed, long-hafted greataxes: overhand chops, level sweeps and ground slams. |
   | `Scholar` (Mage) | **Staff (`STAFF`)** | Ranged mystic projectiles and aura empowerment. |
   | `Healer` | **Tome (`TOME`)** | Channeled magical bursts and restorative resonance. |

   Swords and shields are a single combined weapon; legacy `SWORD` and `SHIELD` artifacts migrate to `SWORD_SHIELD`. Rings (`RING`) are removed: owned rings are deleted on load, and any hero left holding a weapon its archetype can no longer use is unequipped and given a compatible unequipped artifact if one is owned.
2. **Prefab Directory:** Centralized in `ReplicatedStorage.Assets.Weapons` (`weapon_bow`, `weapon_sword_shield`, `weapon_daggers`, `weapon_axe`, `weapon_staff`, `weapon_tome`; `weapon_sword` remains as the bare blade the sword & shield prefab is built from).
3. **Physical Constraints:** All weapon parts have `CanCollide = false`, `CanTouch = false`, `CanQuery = false`, `Massless = true`, `Anchored = false`, welded to the prefab's PrimaryPart `Handle`.
4. **Dynamic Socketing:** Each prefab carries oriented socket attachments: `Grip` on every weapon, plus `ShieldMount` on the shield and `Nock`/`TipUpper`/`TipLower` on the bow. The client joins each one to the matching hero-rig attachment with a single `Weld` (`HeroSockets`).

   | Weapon type | Main hand | Second socket |
   | --- | --- | --- |
   | `BOW` | Left hand grip | — (string `Nock` and limb tips for the draw) |
   | `SWORD_SHIELD` | Right hand grip | Shield strapped to the outer left forearm |
   | `DAGGERS` | Right hand grip | Off-hand dagger in the left hand grip |
   | `AXE` | Right hand grip, high on the haft below the head | Left hand on `OffhandGrip`, 1.7 studs towards the butt. Not welded: `HeroAnimator.SolveOffhand` solves the left arm onto it every frame (`HeroSockets.OFFHAND`) |
   | `STAFF` | Right hand grip | — |
   | `TOME` | Left hand grip | — |

   Hero bodies never carry weapons or shields of their own; the equipped artifact is the only weapon shown.

---

### 7.6 Weapon Reforge (Runesmith's Anvil)
A repeatable Gold sink that improves a weapon without raising its rarity or stars. The **Runesmith's Anvil** stands on the Cloister Commons west of the Reflecting Pool, mirroring the Raid Portal (`HubLayout.ReforgeStation`, built at runtime by `ReforgeService.BuildStation`, so it needs no hub rebake). Its prompt opens the Reforge window (`ReforgeController`); every action goes through `ReforgeRemotes.ReforgeRequest` and is validated on the server (`ReforgeService`, rules in `Util/ReforgeRules`, data in `Config/ReforgeConfig`).
- **One enchantment per weapon.** A roll picks one of four enchantments evenly and a grade by weight. Values are percent by grade (Common / Uncommon / Rare / Epic / Legendary / Godly):

  | Enchantment | Effect | Values |
  | --- | --- | --- |
  | Keen | damage | 2 / 4 / 6 / 9 / 13 / 20 |
  | Vigor | HP | 2 / 3 / 5 / 7 / 10 / 15 |
  | Swift | attack speed (added to the weapon's speed bonus) | 2 / 3 / 5 / 7 / 10 / 14 |
  | Focus | Skill Gauge charge | 4 / 6 / 9 / 12 / 16 / 25 |

- **Grade odds:** Common 50%, Uncommon 28%, Rare 14%, Epic 6%, Legendary 1.8%, Godly 0.2%, shown in the window before any roll. **Pity:** after 49 rolls in a row below Epic (counted per Master, across all weapons), the next roll is Epic or better.
- **Keep or discard.** A roll charges its Gold at once and shows the result next to the current enchantment; the Master keeps the new enchantment or the old one. Rolling again on the same weapon discards the waiting result; another weapon cannot be rolled until it is resolved. The waiting result is saved, so a disconnect never loses a paid roll.
- **Cost per roll:** `5,000 × rarity Gold factor × (1 + 0.5 × (stars − 1))`, the same rarity factor as promotion (§4.2a): 5,000 for a Common 1★, 1.2M for a Mythical 7★.
- **Power is capped on purpose.** The best enchantment is +20% damage or +15% HP, while one hero star is ×3.24, so an enchantment never replaces a promotion and the floor-50 gate (§2.5) still needs 7★ heroes. Enchantments apply before Rift Attunement (`StatFormulas.GetCombatSnapshot`), so the cap still holds.
- **Promotion and selling.** A promoted weapon keeps its enchantment; donors and sold weapons lose theirs. The Forge's sell confirmation must be held for a weapon with an Epic or better enchantment, and the upgrade review says "reforged enchantments lost" when a donor has one.
- **Save:** `artifact.Enchantment = { Id, Grade }` and `profile.Reforge = { Pity, Rolls, Pending }`, both optional; `ReforgeRules.EnsureState` adds the state to old saves and drops damaged values on load, so no schema migration is needed.

## 8. NETWORK PROTOCOL & REPLICATION ARCHITECTURE

### 8.1 Room-Local Replication v2 Protocol
To isolate simulation state and prevent entity overflow or vertical AoE bleed across the 50 Tower Floors:
- **Packet Structure:** 46-byte header + 24 bytes per entity record. Batches capped at 32 records (814 bytes total).
- **Context Identity:** `(ServerSessionId, FloorId, RoomGeneration, ViewEpoch)`.
  - Floor 0 represents the Central Hub and Fortress Plots.
  - Floor 60 is the off-map raid arena (§5.6). Only a server-issued raid pass opens it, and tower-only systems (floor selector, runs, kill progress, relics, nav graphs) skip it.
  - Floors 1–50 represent dynamic isolated tower chambers (with Biome Boss arenas on Floors 5, 10, 15... 50).
- **Entity Record Layout:**
  - `EntityId` (u32), `EntityGeneration` (u32).
  - Local quantized `X, Y, Z` (3x i16, factor 10.0).
  - `Yaw` (u8), `PresentationState` (u8), `HP Permille` (u16, 0..1000), `Stamina` (u8, 0..100).
  - `Flags` (u8: bit 0 hero, bit 1 overdrive, bit 2 pacified, bit 3 boss core exposed).
  - `Current ActionSequence` (u32).
- **Hero Attack Cue (`HERO_CUE`):** A cosmetic, room-scoped 44-byte buffer sent on the unreliable `CombatVFX` remote when a hero decides an attack (`TowerProtocol.EncodeHeroCue` / `DecodeHeroCue`). Damage never depends on it: a lost cue only loses the animation and effect.
  - `Tag` (u8 = 7), `Tier` (u8, 1 Basic / 2 Special / 3 Skill), `Flags` (u8: bit 0 overdrive), padding (u8).
  - Attacker `EntityId` and `EntityGeneration` (u32 each), target `EntityId` (u32, 0 = none).
  - `CastMs`, `ReleaseMs`, `TravelMs` (u16 each, from `HeroActionTiming`), padding (u16).
  - `StartServerTime` (f64, `GetServerTimeNow()` at the decision), world aim point (3x f32).
  - The client drops malformed cues and cues for a stale generation. A late cue starts the clip `GetServerTimeNow() - StartServerTime` seconds in, and a cue older than its cast time is ignored.
- **Roster bootstraps:** The reliable `TowerEntityState` BOOTSTRAP/COMPLETE pair carries the full roster (metadata) of the receiver's view. Tower roster changes (kills, camp waves, boss summons, surges, hero level-ups) are queued per floor and sent once per frame, only to the players whose view is on that floor, and always before the next snapshot (`CombatSimulation.queueFloorBootstrap`). Squad rebuilds, duels and rivals still bootstrap every player at once.
- **Resync:** A client asks for a resync (`EntityMetadataSync`, at most once a second) when a snapshot names an entity it has no metadata for, when an entity has gone a second without a snapshot, or when a snapshot's `ActionSequence` is still ahead of the actions it has received after 0.5 s (snapshots are unreliable and actions reliable, so a snapshot often lands just before its START). The server answers with a `Context` COMMIT of the unchanged view (`RequestId = "ENTITY_RESYNC"`) and a bootstrap. Clients reconcile in place on that COMMIT: they do not clear the view or touch the Master's character.

### 8.2 Reliable Travel & Squad Assignment Lifecycle
- **Transition Lane:** One serialized transition lane per player. State transitions (`VISIT`, `HUB`, `ASSIGN`, `RECALL`) are verified against character generation and monotonic location/squad revisions.
- **Idempotency & Replay Cache:** The server retains the last 64 terminal request results for 60 seconds per connection.
- **Budgeting:** Token bucket burst 4, refill 2 tokens/second per owner. 8-second preparation deadline before timeout rollback.

### 8.3 Profile Persistence & Session Locking
- **Store:** One DataStore key per player, `PlayerProfiles_v5` / `User_{UserId}`, linked to the UserId for right-to-be-forgotten. The name is versioned so all progress can be reset by bumping it (v4 to v5 on 2026-11-17 wiped every profile; the old store is left in place). The record is `{ Data, Lock = { JobId, Time }, SavedAt }`. Every write goes through `UpdateAsync` (`ProfilePersistence`, with the pure lock rules in `ProfileCodec`).
- **Join:** The server claims the lock, then runs the saved profile (or a fresh default for a new player) through `PitService.EnsureDefaults`, `EnsureFacilities`, `ProfileMigrations.Run`, `OnboardingService.EnsureState`, `FortressTreeService.ApplyLevels`, `RewardsService.EnsureState`, `PotionService.EnsureState` and `BattlePassService.EnsureState`. `SyncProfile` lazy migrations still run on every sync. A lock held by another live server is retried 6 times, 5 seconds apart, before the player is kicked with "rejoin". A lock not refreshed for 10 minutes belongs to a crashed server and is taken over.
- **Saves:** Autosave every 120–135 seconds per player (which refreshes the lock), on `PlayerRemoving` (saves and releases), and in `BindToClose` (up to 25 seconds). `DataManager.SaveProfile(player)` is the checkpoint save for purchases; it does nothing once the player's session is released. A save is cancelled unless the record still holds this server's lock, so a stale server can never overwrite newer progress, and a save that lands after the release (an autosave retrying, a purchase checkpoint) can never lock the record again and keep the player out until the lock times out. If a save finds another server holds the lock, this server stops saving the player and kicks them with "Your save was opened on another server. Please rejoin." Failures retry with exponential backoff.
- **Schema:** `ProfileMigrations.CurrentSchema` is 11. v11 adds `profile.BattlePass`, `profile.Potions` and `profile.Boosts` (§4.9): older saves start the current season at level 0 with no potions and no running boosts. v10 adds `profile.Notoriety` (§6.1) with zeros (lifetime `Thefts`, `BountiesClaimed`, `Escapes`; the per-victim daily counts; this week's totals); `NotorietyRules.Ensure` repairs a broken one. v9 removes Soul Essence and turns any held essence into Promotion Stones at the old crafting rate (8 per stone, rounded up, no Gold or Gems charged). v8 adds `profile.Rewards` (§4.8) with nothing claimed. v3–v5 cover onboarding, 1★ summons and per-star levels (§4.1), v6 adds the onboarding guide, and v7 deletes the retired social systems (`profile.Social`: pacts, mercenaries, mimic chests, plot rune espionage) and their facility fields (`UncollectedOutput`, `BlessedUntil`, `LeechedUntil`, `LeechVisitorUserId`, `LastRuneTimestamp`, `LastRuneWasLeech`). It also removes Pit imprints (`InheritedImprints`, `ImprintCapacity`, `SynthesisHistory.ImprintsPerformed`), refunds each held Imprint Catalyst as 150 Gold, 15 Gems and 4 Soul Essence, and drops each hero's display-only `PassiveTrait` name.
- **Robux receipts:** `ShopService.ProcessReceipt` records each granted `PurchaseId` in `profile.PurchaseIds` (the last 100, newest last) together with the grant, then saves the profile and answers `PurchaseGranted` only if that save succeeds. The grant and its `PurchaseId` are saved or lost together, so a failed save or a crash makes Roblox retry the receipt and it is granted exactly once. A retry of a receipt whose grant is still running is answered `NotProcessedYet`. In Studio without API access the purchase is confirmed without a save.
- **What is saved:** A sanitised deep copy. Derived fields (`SquadPower`, `SquadPowerByHero`) are stripped, functions and Instances are dropped, and NaN/inf numbers become 0.
- **Studio:** Without "Enable Studio Access to API Services" the server warns and runs unsaved sessions instead of kicking. Studio play sessions use `studio-` JobIds and may take over a lock left by an earlier Studio session (Stop often kills the server before the release lands); they never take over a live server's lock, and live servers never take over a fresh Studio lock.
- **Shutdown:** `BindToClose` waits (up to 25 s) for every in-flight release, including the ones `PlayerRemoving` started.

### 8.4 Profile Sync (Deltas)
- **What travels:** `SyndicateRemotes.ProfileDeltaSync` carries the whole profile once (the first sync after join, the first after each respawn, and on request) and a delta every time after that. A client that gets a delta with no profile to apply it to fires `SyndicateRemotes.RequestProfileResync` (no arguments, at most once per 5 s per player) and the server sends the whole profile again. This happens when the first profile arrives before `HUDController` is listening: a RemoteEvent fired before any listener is queued for the first listener only. The server keeps a copy of what each client last received, diffs the live profile against it and sends only the difference; nothing is sent when nothing changed (`Shared/Util/ProfileDelta`).
- **Delta shape:** `{ _Delta = true, Set = { key = value }, Unset = { key }, Maps = { Heroes = { Set, Unset }, Artifacts = { Set, Unset } } }`. Top-level keys are resent whole; `Heroes` and `Artifacts` are diffed entry by entry. Applying a delta twice equals applying it once.
- **Three server entry points:** `DataManager.SyncProfile` (lazy migrations, squad rebuild, plot refresh, then send), `DataManager.SyncPitDelta` (hero stat refresh, then send) and `DataManager.SyncLight` (send only). `SyncLight` is for frequent changes that only move displayed numbers: kill Gold and Gems, passive Gold and stamina. Anything that changes the squad, equipment, facilities or hero stats must use `SyncProfile`. The 1 Hz economy loop sends a light delta every 3 s and a full `SyncProfile` every 30 s or when a hero leaves a coma.
- **Client:** `HUDController` owns the cached profile (`_G.CurrentProfile`) and applies deltas to it in place. Other scripts only fill `_G.CurrentProfile` while it is still empty. Panels refresh only when a sync touched a key they depend on (`PANEL_IGNORES` lists what each panel does not depend on, so a new profile field refreshes too much rather than too little), and delta-driven refreshes are merged and run once per 0.2 s. A whole profile refreshes everything at once.

### 8.5 Hub Streaming & Baked Scenery
- **Streaming:** `StreamingTargetRadius` is 500 studs (set in the place). Nothing on a plot is `Persistent` except its small `PlotBeaconAnchor`, so clients stream other players' plots by distance (`PlotManager`). The island (walls, courtyard, outskirts) is `Default`, so its parts arrive a few at a time; the fountain, the statue and each building are `Atomic`, because client effects bind to their children as soon as the model appears. The owner keeps their own plot loaded through a replication focus on the plot's anchor. The join loading screen streams the whole hub in and stays up until it has arrived (every plot, the Commons and the tower plaza, `HubLayout.StreamPreloadPoints`), and `StreamOutBehavior` is `LowMemory` (set in the place), so walking the hub does not hitch on streaming bursts; content is only dropped when the device runs low on memory. Client code must treat another plot's buildings as possibly absent.
- **Unoccupied plots:** a plot with no owner has no buildings. Every `Building_*` model in `PlotBuildings` (the Guard Post included) and the plaza fountain are taken out of the DataModel until a Master is assigned the plot, and put back when one is (`FacilityPresence`). The fortress walls and gatehouses, the courtyard (floor, lanterns, benches) and the outskirts scenery stay.
- **Baked scenery:** `FortressWalls` (about 2,200 plain parts per plot) and `CourtyardGroundTiles` (246) are merged in Blender into one mesh per material and colour (`tools/blender/bake_scenery_meshes.py`, 12 and 10 meshes). When the imported templates exist in `ReplicatedStorage.Assets.BakedScenery`, `SceneryBake.Apply` removes the baked parts and places the meshes; a baked part that collides stays as an invisible collider, so walking and raycasts are unchanged. Parts with lights, emitters or attachments, and anything transparent, stay real parts. Each bake records a signature of the parts it replaces: if a builder changes, the signature no longer matches and the parts are kept until the bake is re-run. Importing or re-baking a template rebuilds the baked hub once on the next server start.
